### [1] [GeoUniPR: A Geometry-Consistent Unified Framework for Cross-Modal Place Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11263)
*Wonbong Kim,Jiatong Xiao,Rui Li,Xufei Wang,Qiwen Gu,Junqiao Zhao,Chen Ye,Guang Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出GeoUniPR，一个统一且简洁的几何一致性跨模态位置识别框架。通过将LiDAR点云投影到相机视角构建几何一致的深度图像视图（DIV），并融入强度与表面法线信息形成多通道几何表示，利用两个结构相同的ViT编码器（参数高效微调）学习统一嵌入空间，配合新提出的SC-InfoNCE对比损失抑制距离导致的假负样本，在KITTI和KITTI-360上取得SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有跨模态位置识别方法通常依赖复杂的对齐模块、多阶段训练或对预训练骨干网络的完全微调，计算成本高且流程繁琐。作者希望从几何一致性角度出发，设计一个更简洁统一的框架，减少跨模态差异并提升识别性能。

Method: (1) 将LiDAR点云投影到相机视角构建几何一致的深度图像视图（DIV），建立RGB-LiDAR直接对应关系；(2) 在DIV上融合原生LiDAR强度与表面法线信息，形成多通道几何表示增强结构一致性；(3) 使用两个结构相同的ViT编码器学习统一嵌入空间，仅采用参数高效微调，无需辅助对齐模块、多阶段训练或全量微调；(4) 提出SC-InfoNCE对比损失，抑制空间连续条件下距离导致的假负样本。

Result: 在KITTI和KITTI-360数据集上，GeoUniPR在相同模态和跨模态位置识别任务中均达到SOTA性能，并展现出强大的跨数据集泛化能力。

Conclusion: 该工作验证了从表示层面通过几何一致性构建跨模态对应关系的有效性，证明了参数高效微调策略足以在跨模态位置识别中达到甚至超越复杂对齐方法的性能，为CMPR提供了一种更简洁高效的解决范式。

Abstract: Cross-modal place recognition (CMPR) aims to identify the same location across heterogeneous sensing modalities, such as vision and LiDAR. Existing methods commonly bridge the modality gap using complex alignment modules, multi-stage training, or full fine-tuning of pretrained backbones. In this work, we revisit CMPR from the perspective of geometric consistency and propose GeoUniPR, a unified and concise geometry-consistent framework. GeoUniPR reduces cross-modal discrepancy at the representation level by projecting LiDAR point clouds into the camera perspective to construct Geometry-Consistent depth image views (DIV), which establish direct RGB-LiDAR correspondence. We further augment DIV with native LiDAR cues, including intensity and surface-normal information, yielding a multi-channel geometric representation that improves structural consistency. Based on this representation, GeoUniPR learns a unified embedding space using two modality-specific ViT-based encoders with identical architectures, trained through parameter-efficient adaptation without auxiliary alignment modules, multi-stage training, or full backbone fine-tuning. In addition, we introduce Spatially-Consistent InfoNCE (SC-InfoNCE), a CMPR-specific contrastive objective that suppresses distance-induced false negatives under spatial continuity. Extensive experiments on KITTI and KITTI-360 demonstrate that GeoUniPR achieves state-of-the-art (SOTA) performance in both same-modal and cross-modal place recognition, with strong cross-dataset generalization.

</details>


### [2] [SegPAR: Class-Centric Decision-Based Sparse Attack for Semantic Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11285)
*Dongsu Song,DaeYun GO,Boseung Seo,Jay Hoon Jung*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文针对语义分割中稀疏决策型黑盒攻击研究不足的问题，提出SegPAR框架，通过类中心探索范式和差异奖励机制，显著提升了攻击的稀疏效率和MIoU降低效果，性能接近白盒攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏决策型黑盒威胁在语义分割中的研究十分有限，现有方法存在严重的查询效率问题，因为图像中心的像素累积策略会在巨大的图像空间内迅速耗尽查询预算。

Method: 将分类领域的代表性决策型黑盒稀疏攻击适配到语义分割作为基线，并提出SegPAR框架，采用类中心探索范式替代图像中心像素累积，同时引入差异奖励来消除标准决策奖励在像素累积过程中产生的误导性反馈。

Result: 大量实验表明SegPAR在稀疏效率和MIoU降低方面显著优于黑盒基线，并且与白盒稀疏攻击性能相当。

Conclusion: SegPAR填补了语义分割中稀疏决策型黑盒攻击研究的空白，通过类中心探索和差异奖励两个关键设计实现了高效的攻击性能，并为该领域建立了严格的基准。

Abstract: Despite the practical relevance of sparse decision-based black-box threats, they have received limited attention in semantic segmentation. To bridge this gap, we adapt the most representative decision-based black-box sparse attacks from the classification domain to serve as baselines, establishing a rigorous benchmark for this underexplored setting. In this context, we demonstrate that one of the existing methods suffers from severe query inefficiency due to its image-centric pixel accumulation, which rapidly exhausts query budgets across the vast image space. To overcome this, we propose SegPAR, a novel decision-based framework that shifts to a class-centric exploration paradigm. Furthermore, to eliminate the misleading feedback generated by standard decision rewards during pixel accumulation, we introduce a novel discrepancy reward. Extensive experiments show that SegPAR significantly outperforms black-box baselines in sparsity efficiency and MIoU reduction, while remaining competitive with white-box sparse attacks. Code is available at \href{https://github.com/KAU-QuantumAILab/SegPAR}{https://github.com/KAU-QuantumAILab/SegPAR}.

</details>


### [3] [CLEAR: Class-wise Expert Aggregation with Structured Sampling for Long-Tailed Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11287)
*Gawon Lim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出CLEAR框架，通过阈值化结构化采样生成多样专家，利用平滑的类级精度估计每个专家的可信度分数，并采用类级广义专家乘积聚合（PoE）进行推理，在多个长尾数据集上取得了有竞争力的准确性，尤其在少样本类别上表现突出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长尾分类中，模型在频繁类别和稀有类别上的可靠性不均匀，现有方法虽通过多种策略处理不平衡问题，但很少显式估计每个类别应信任哪个专家，缺乏类级可靠性感知。

Method: CLEAR包含三个核心组件：(1) 通过阈值化结构化采样生成多样专家，同时保留完整标签空间；(2) 使用平滑的类级精度公式估计每个专家对每个类别的信任分数；(3) 推理时通过类级广义专家乘积（product-of-experts）聚合专家预测，使不同专家在不同类别上获得不同权重。

Result: 在CIFAR-100-LT、ImageNet-LT和Places-LT数据集上的多个骨干网络实验中，CLEAR取得了有竞争力的总体准确率，尤其在少样本（few-shot）类别上表现突出，验证了类级专家可靠性作为长尾集成学习设计原则的有效性。

Conclusion: 类级专家可靠性是一个有用的长尾集成学习设计原则，CLEAR通过模块化的专家聚合框架有效提升了长尾分类的整体及少数类性能。

Abstract: Long-tailed classification poses a reliability challenge because models trained on imbalanced data are unevenly reliable across frequent and underrepresented classes. While existing methods address imbalance through re-balancing, adjustment, representation learning, or multi-expert modeling, they rarely estimate which expert should be trusted for each class. This paper proposes CLEAR (Class-wise reLiability-aware Expert Aggregation for long-tailed Recognition), a modular ensemble framework for long-tailed classification. CLEAR generates diverse experts through threshold-based structured sampling while preserving the full label space, then estimates a class-wise trust score for each expert using a smoothed class-wise precision formulation. During inference, expert predictions are combined through class-wise generalized product-of-experts aggregation, allowing different experts to be emphasized for different classes. Experiments on CIFAR-100-LT, ImageNet-LT, and Places-LT across multiple backbones show that CLEAR achieves competitive overall accuracy and particularly strong few-shot performance. These results support class-wise expert reliability as a useful design principle for long-tailed ensemble learning.

</details>


### [4] [Self-Evolving Code-with-Image Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11292)
*Tianze Yang,Liang Wu,Ruitong Sun,Yucheng Shi,Yanqiao Wang,Mayank Darbari,Ninghao Liu,Jin Sun,Liangjie Hong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Code-with-Image范式，让多模态模型仅凭Python解释器在代码中实现真正的视觉算法来解决视觉问题，并引入训练无关的反思循环，让模型通过研究自己的失败程序、测试修复方案来进化可迁移的技能。在CwI-Bench基准上，GPT-5.6-luna从工具无关思维链的<30%提升到43%（裸解释器），再提升到67%（技能进化）；开源27B模型从9%攀升到56%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有"思考与图像"范式（多模态模型通过裁剪、缩放、旋转等工具辅助视觉任务）主要受制于感知瓶颈，推理仍停留在语言层面。然而某些视觉问题并非感知受限，而是需要执行多步视觉算法，模型虽能说出正确算法却无法真正运行它——语言能描述算法，但无法执行算法。这一差距正是该工作的核心动机。

Method: 1) 提出Code-with-Image范式：仅给模型一个Python解释器，要求其编写代码实现真正的视觉算法，程序本身即推理过程。2) 引入训练无关的自我反思循环（reflection loop）：模型研究自己失败的程序，针对构造性真值测试修复方案，保留通过测试的技能作为可移植能力。3) 构建CwI-Bench基准：30个任务族，由隐藏的视觉计算诱导生成，具有不相交的学习和评估划分。

Result: 在CwI-Bench上：GPT-5.6-luna在使用工具无关思维链时低于30%，使用裸解释器时达到43%，通过自身可执行反思进化技能后达到67%。开源27B模型经历了类似的阶梯式提升（9%→33%→56%），且进化出的技能是纯文本形式，可以跨模型规模和任务族迁移。

Conclusion: 当代码承载推理时，调试代码就等同于调试推理。这一范式将瓶颈从"执行代码"转移到"决定实现哪种算法"，并通过让模型自我教学的方式有效提升复杂视觉算法的解决能力。技能的可迁移性和纯文本特性表明该方法具有广泛适用性。

Abstract: Multimodal models increasingly reach for tools when solving visual tasks (crop, zoom, rotate, brighten), a paradigm known as thinking-with-images. The central challenge is one of perception: tools mostly serve to expose visual evidence, reasoning over that evidence stays in language, and most targets are ones a human could in principle determine by inspection. Some visual questions, however, are not bottlenecked by perception: recovering their answers requires executing a multi-step visual algorithm over the pixels. On such questions a model often names the correct algorithm at once yet still answers wrong, because language can describe an algorithm without being able to run one. Code-with-Image crosses that line: given nothing but a Python interpreter, the model must implement a genuine visual algorithm in code to solve the task; the program itself becomes the reasoning. The bottleneck then shifts from executing code to deciding which algorithm to implement. So we let the model teach itself: a training-free reflection loop studies its own failed programs, tests repairs against constructive ground truth, and keeps what survives as portable skills. On our Code-with-Image Bench (CwI-Bench), thirty task families induced by hidden visual computations with disjoint learning and evaluation splits, even GPT-5.6-luna stays below 30% with tool-free chain of thought; given a bare interpreter it reaches 43%, and with skills evolved through its own executable reflection, 67%. The open 27B model climbs the same ladder (9% $\rightarrow$ 33% $\rightarrow$ 56%), and the skills are plain text, transferable across scales and families. When code carries the reasoning, debugging code becomes debugging reasoning.

</details>


### [5] [Clinical Feasibility of Low-Magnification Fluorescence Imaging for Breast Cancer Margin Detection Using Texture Analysis and Deep Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11317)
*Pouya Afshin,Tianling Niu,Tongtong Lu,David Helminiak,Julie Jorns,Mollie Patton,Tina Yen,Donghye Ye,Bing Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究比较了MUSE技术在4x和10x放大倍数下对乳腺癌手术切缘评估的性能，使用LBP纹理分析和ViT深度学习两种方法，发现两种放大倍数均能达到相当高的诊断准确率（约96-98%），且4x放大倍数提供了更大的视野和更快的成像速度，可有效用于术中切缘评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 乳腺癌手术中快速准确地评估手术切缘状态至关重要。MUSE技术有望用于术中切缘检查，但不同放大倍数下的诊断性能尚需系统比较，以确定最优成像策略。

Method: 对MUSE获取的4x和10x放大倍数图像进行patch级别分类。采用两种方法：基于局部二值模式（LBP）的纹理分析（TA）和基于Vision Transformer（ViT）基础模型的深度学习（DL）方法，分别在两种放大倍数下评估分类性能。

Result: DL方法在4x和10x下均达到96.30%灵敏度、100%特异性和98.18%准确率。TA方法在4x下特异性更高（100% vs 93.33%），10x下灵敏度更高（100% vs 93.33%），但两者准确率相同（96.67%）。10x放大倍数未带来明显性能提升。

Conclusion: 4x和10x放大倍数在MUSE系统切缘评估中具有同等的诊断准确率，而4x具备更大的视野和更快的成像速度，因此低放大倍数可有效用于准确高效的术中切缘评估。

Abstract: High-resolution images of unprocessed surgical breast tissue can be obtained using microscopy with ultraviolet surface excitation (MUSE). This technique is considered a promising method for checking surgical margins during breast cancer surgery. In this study, MUSE images at 4x and 10x magnifications were compared using patch-level classification methods. Texture analysis (TA) based on local binary patterns (LBP) and deep learning (DL) with a base Vision Transformer (ViT) model were used. Both methods achieved similar performance at both magnifications. Using DL method, both 4x and 10x magnifications achieved 96.30% sensitivity, 100% specificity and 98.18% accuracy. Using TA method, 4x achieved better specificity (100% vs 93.33%) and 10x yielded higher sensitivity (100% vs 93.33%), but both had the same accuracy (96.67%). No clear improvement in performance was observed with 10x magnification. These results show that 4x imaging achieves the same diagnostic accuracy as 10x imaging. At the same time, 4x offers a larger field of view and faster image capture. Therefore, lower magnification can be effectively used in MUSE systems for accurate and efficient intraoperative margin assessment.

</details>


### [6] [Dual-Domain Cross-Modal Decoding for Clinical Text-Guided Medical Image Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11335)
*Md Maklachur Rahman,Tracy Hammond*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出DD-CMD（双域跨模态解码）方法，用于临床文本引导的肺部感染分割。该方法在解码过程中同时整合空间域和频率域两种互补的语言引导：空间域通过TGSA对齐多尺度视觉特征与文本语义，频率域通过STAM利用2D DCT和FiLM参数实现频率感知的通道重校准。在两个公开数据集上取得了优于现有方法的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本引导的分割方法主要关注空间对齐，却忽略了控制纹理和边界的频率信息。临床文本虽然能缩小分割范围，但单纯依赖空间域引导无法充分利用频率域中蕴含的细节信息，导致分割性能受限。

Method: DD-CMD包含两个核心模块：(1) 空间域中的TGSA（Text-Guided Spatial Cross-Attention），用于对齐多尺度视觉token与文本语义，并通过门控残差融合更新特征；(2) 频率域中的STAM（Spectral-Text Adaptive Modulation），利用2D DCT计算可学习的频带能量统计，并预测文本条件的FiLM参数来重校准解码器通道。两个模块嵌入粗到细的解码器（7x7到56x56）中，通过轻量级两阶段细化模块恢复全分辨率掩码。

Result: 在QaTa-COV19数据集上达到91.46% Dice和84.26% mIoU，在MosMedData+数据集上达到81.95% Dice和69.42% mIoU，相比最强基线平均提升+1.96 Dice和+2.67 mIoU。

Conclusion: DD-CMD通过同时利用空间域和频率域的语言引导，有效解决了文本引导分割中频率信息被忽视的问题，验证了双域跨模态解码策略在临床肺部感染分割任务中的有效性。

Abstract: Clinical text can narrow down what to segment, but recent text-guided designs emphasize spatial alignment while overlooking frequency content that governs texture and boundaries. We propose Dual-Domain Cross-Modal Decoding (DD-CMD) for clinical text-guided pulmonary infection segmentation, integrating two complementary forms of language guidance during decoding. In the spatial domain, Text-Guided Spatial Cross-Attention (TGSA) aligns multi-scale visual tokens with text semantics and updates features through gated residual fusion. In the frequency domain, Spectral-Text Adaptive Modulation (STAM) applies a 2D DCT to compute learnable band-energy statistics and predicts text-conditioned FiLM parameters to recalibrate decoder channels for frequency-aware decoding. DD-CMD embeds TGSA and STAM into a coarse-to-fine decoder (7x7 to 56x56) and restores full-resolution masks using a lightweight two-stage refinement module. Experiments on QaTa-COV19 and MosMedData+ show that DD-CMD achieves 91.46% Dice / 84.26% mIoU and 81.95% Dice / 69.42% mIoU, respectively, with average gains of +1.96 Dice and +2.67 mIoU over the strongest prior baselines. Code: https://github.com/maklachur/DD-CMD.

</details>


### [7] [Gaze Target Estimation Anywhere with Concepts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11367)
*Xu Cao,Houze Yang,Vipin Gunda,Zhongyi Zhou,Tianyu Xu,Adarsh Kowdle,Inki Kim,James M. Rehg*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了Promptable Gaze Target Estimation (PGE)任务，一种基于自然语言或视觉提示的端到端视线目标估计新范式。作者开发了数据引擎生成120K图像的Gaze-Co数据集，并提出GazeAnywhere模型，该模型使用基于Transformer的检测器融合冻结编码器特征，同时解决主体定位、画面内外存在性和视线目标热图估计，在多个PGE基准上达到最先进性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视线目标估计方法依赖多阶段流程（如头部边界框和人体姿态等中间输入），存在错误级联问题，且无法通过自然语言提示灵活指定视线分析任务，缺乏可扩展性和便利性。

Method: 提出PGE任务范式，通过文本或视觉提示（如"穿红衣服的男孩"或"位于[0.52,0.48]的人"）端到端地指定视线分析主体。开发数据引擎生成Gaze-Co数据集（120K带提示标注的图像对），并提出GazeAnywhere模型——基于Transformer的检测器，融合冻结编码器特征，同时解决主体定位、帧内/帧外存在性和视线目标热图估计三个子任务。

Result: GazeAnywhere在多个PGE基准上达到最先进性能，即使在困难的跨域真实临床数据集上也展现了良好的泛化能力，为PGE新问题设立了强基线。

Conclusion: 通过概念驱动的提示范式，PGE和GazeAnywhere解决了传统多阶段流程的级联错误问题，实现了更灵活、更可扩展的视线目标估计，并已开源。

Abstract: Estimating human gaze targets from images in-the-wild is an important and formidable task. Existing approaches primarily employ brittle, multi-stage pipelines that require explicit inputs, like head bounding boxes and human pose, in order to identify the subject of gaze analysis. As a result, detection errors can cascade and lead to failure. Moreover, these prior works lack the flexibility of specifying the gaze analysis task via natural language prompting, an approach which has been shown to have significant benefits in convenience and scalability for other image analysis tasks. To overcome these limitations, we introduce the Promptable Gaze Target Estimation (PGE) task, a new end-to-end, concept-driven paradigm for gaze analysis. PGE conditions gaze prediction on flexible user text or visual prompts (e.g., "the boy in the red shirt" or "person in point [0.52, 0.48]") to identify a specific subject for gaze analysis. This approach integrates subject localization with gaze estimation, and eliminates the rigid dependency on intermediate analysis stages. We develop a scalable data engine to generate Gaze-Co (Gaze Estimation with Concepts), a dataset and benchmark of 120K high-quality, prompt-annotated image pairs. We also propose GazeAnywhere, the first model designed for PGE. GazeAnywhere uses a transformer-based detector to fuse features from frozen encoders and simultaneously solves subject localization, in/out-of-frame presence, and gaze target heatmap estimation. GazeAnywhere achieves state-of-the-art performance on multiple PGE benchmarks, setting a strong baseline for this new problem even on a difficult out-of-domain, real-world clinical dataset. GazeAnywhere is open-sourced in github.com/IrohXu/GazeAnywhere.

</details>


### [8] [COGENT: Counterfactual Gaussian Explanations for Volumetric Medical Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11422)
*Dorian Rząsa,Bartosz Zabdyr,Krzysztof Piekarz,Jakub Grzywaczewski,Bartlomiej Sobieski,Przemyslaw Biecek,Żaneta Świderska-Chadaj,Olga Śliwicka,Przemysław Spurek,Joanna Świebocka-Więk*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出COGENT框架，在高斯体素表示参数空间中生成反事实解释，用于医学体积图像深度学习模型的可解释性分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有医学体积成像的可解释性方法主要在体素空间操作，忽略了3D场景建模引入的结构化表示，难以提供符合解剖学一致性的解释。

Method: 基于MedGS和Sybil肺癌风险预测模型，在高斯体素表示参数空间中通过可微渲染管线优化高斯基元，将可解释性转化为显式3D场景表示上的反事实优化问题。

Result: 在肺CT扫描上通过定量比较和医学专家定性分析验证，表明表示空间的反事实优化提供了临床有意义的解释。

Conclusion: 表示空间反事实优化为解释体积深度学习模型提供了新视角，生成稀疏且空间局部化的解释，同时保持解剖学一致性。

Abstract: Explainability is essential for deploying deep learning models in high-stakes medical applications. Existing explainability methods for volumetric imaging predominantly operate in voxel space, overlooking the structured representations introduced by recent advances in 3D scene modeling. We present COGENT (Counterfactual Gaussian Explanations), a framework that generates counterfactual explanations directly in the parameter space of Gaussian-based volumetric representations. Built upon MedGS and the Sybil lung cancer risk prediction model, COGENT optimizes selected Gaussian primitives through a differentiable rendering pipeline, enabling gradients from the downstream predictor to identify representation components that most influence model decisions. Unlike conventional pixel- or voxel-level attribution methods, our approach formulates explainability as a counterfactual optimization problem over an explicit 3D scene representation, producing sparse and spatially localized explanations while preserving anatomical consistency. We evaluate COGENT on lung CT scans using quantitative comparisons with existing explainability methods together with qualitative analysis by medical experts. The results demonstrate that representation-space counterfactual optimization provides clinically meaningful explanations while offering a new perspective on interpreting volumetric deep learning models.

</details>


### [9] [VLMs Win a Systematic Evaluation of Underwater Image Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11425)
*Sara Aghajanzadeh,Yingxue Wang,Ieva Bagdonaviciute,David Forsyth*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个系统化的水下图像复原评估流程，用于衡量方法的准确性、跨相机移动的重建一致性以及水参数的影响，并用该流程评估了从显式物理散射模型到视觉语言模型（VLM）的各种方法。结果表明VLM在评估中全面且显著优于基于物理的模型，真实水下图像结果也证实了这一结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 水下图像复原领域目前缺乏系统性的评估方法，导致不同方法之间难以公平比较，需要建立一个统一的评估流程来标准化水下重建的性能评价。

Method: 设计了一个系统化的评估流程，包含三个评估维度：重建准确性、跨相机移动的重建一致性、水参数变化的影响。利用该流程对多种现有方法进行对比评估，涵盖基于显式物理散射模型的传统方法以及未经过物理模型训练的视觉语言模型（VLM）。

Result: 视觉语言模型（VLM）在所有评估指标上整体且显著优于基于物理模型的方法，这很可能归因于强大的图像先验的重要性。在真实水下场景的图像上进行的实验进一步验证了这一评估结论。

Conclusion: 系统化的评估流程能够有效揭示不同水下复原方法的优劣，而VLM凭借其强大的图像先验在水下图像复原中展现出明显优势，为未来水下图像复原研究提供了新的方向。

Abstract: Underwater image restoration consists of recovering an image which looks like there is no water present. To date, evaluation has not been systematic. This paper describes a systematic evaluation pipeline for underwater reconstruction, which can be used to assess a method for accuracy; consistency of reconstruction over camera moves; and the effect of water parameters. We use this pipeline to evaluate a range of current procedures, from models constructed using explicit but approximate physical models of scattering to Vision-Language Models (VLMs which are not currently trained with explicit physical models). Overall, VLMs wholly and significantly outperform physically based models in our evaluation, likely because of the importance of a strong image prior. Results on images of real underwater scenes strongly confirm the evaluation.

</details>


### [10] [TangPoetryBench: A Multi-Dimensional Benchmark and Rubric-Conditioned Evaluator for Poetry-to-Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11452)
*Haoqi Hu,Tongji Luo,Li Zhang,Boning Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出TangPoetryBench，一个包含1,280张图像（320首唐诗×4个SOTA T2I模型）的多维度基准，并引入PoemAutoEvaluator（PAE）评估器，用于衡量文生图模型对诗歌意境的渲染能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的T2I评估指标（如CLIPScore、BLIPScore、VQAScore）仅能衡量字面图文对应关系，无法评估图像是否真正传达了诗歌的意象与隐含情感，也无法区分最好与最差的模型表现。

Method: 构建包含十个维度的TangPoetryBench基准，对320首唐诗与4个SOTA T2I模型生成的1,280张图像进行质量可控的人工标注；随后提出基于规则的开放评估器PAE，并通过基准数据训练和验证。

Result: PAE与强专有评判器（Claude）达到同等水平，能够泛化到未见过的生成器及第二种诗歌传统（宋词），并支持基准在新图像上无需新人工标注即可扩展。

Conclusion: 该工作揭示了当前T2I模型在诗歌插画任务中共性与模型特异的优缺点，包括对诗歌隐含情感的唤起能力，并提供可扩展的评估框架。

Abstract: Text-to-image (T2I) models are increasingly asked to illustrate literary and cultural content, yet we cannot measure how well an image renders the meaning of a poem. The task is many-sided: a good illustration must be visually sound, faithful to the poem's imagery and scene, culturally and stylistically apt, free of spurious text, and true to its emotion, and its deepest requirements, imagery and especially implicit emotion, are never stated in the words. Existing metrics (CLIPScore, BLIPScore, VQAScore) reward literal text-image correspondence and so cannot tell whether an illustration succeeds, let alone why, or even separate the best model from the worst. We introduce TangPoetryBench, a multi-dimensional benchmark of 1,280 images (320 classical Chinese Tang poems x 4 state-of-the-art T2I models) with quality-controlled human annotations across ten dimensions. Analyzing this data, we reveal the shared and model-specific strengths and weaknesses of current T2I models, including their ability to evoke a poem's implicit emotion. We further introduce PoemAutoEvaluator (PAE), an open, rubric-conditioned evaluator that reaches parity with a strong proprietary judge (Claude), generalizes to an unseen generator and a second poetic tradition (Song Ci), and lets the benchmark scale to new images without fresh human annotation. We release the benchmark, annotations, and evaluator.

</details>


### [11] [Multi-Agent Target-Existence Verification and Learned Mask Geometry Refinement: Winning Report of the MeViS-Text Track at the 8th LSVOS Challenge 2026](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11458)
*Jungyoon Lee,Gyuil Lim,Doeon Kim,Seong-heum Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SSUPER系统在2026年LSVOS挑战赛MeViS-Text赛道中获得第一名，最终得分为0.9081339614。该系统通过三个异构多模态大语言模型协同推理、将存在性验证独立为多智能体审计，并引入仅依赖训练数据的StyleRefiner对齐掩码样式，有效解决了运动表达式引导的视频目标分割和欺骗性无目标表达检测问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对MeViS-Text赛道中由运动表达式引导的参考视频目标分割任务，需要处理包含欺骗性无目标表达（与视频中任何对象都不匹配）的情况。关键难点在于硬负样本通常描述一个看似合理的对象，只有在完整时间谓词下才失败，导致类别上合理的掩码会锚定判定结果，使得无目标表达容易漏网。

Method: SSUPER管线包含三个核心阶段：(1)将每个表达解析为视觉概念，使用SAM 3.1生成全视频候选掩码，并选择目标ID；(2)在每个推理阶段，三个异构多模态大语言模型独立执行相同的阶段特定提示，再通过单一综合过程提交模式验证的判定；(3)将存在性验证独立为多智能体审计，全面检查谓词（类别、数量、动作、轨迹、事件顺序和语义角色），区分缺失与暂时不可见，剔除相机运动造成的表观运动，要求矛盾证据而非单纯不确定性来判定无目标。最终通过仅训练数据的StyleRefiner（零额外的分割调用）对齐掩码几何与MeViSv2的标注样式。

Result: 系统在验证集上拒绝了所有无目标表达，在官方挑战排行榜上达到0.9081339614的最终得分，获得第一名。无分割调用的多智能体审计恢复了大部分残留的无目标错误，StyleRefiner表明在语义确定后部分剩余误差是样式性而非语义性的。

Conclusion: 通过将存在性判定从选择过程中解耦并独立审计完整时间谓词，显著提高了无目标表达检测的鲁棒性。同时证明语义确定性是分割误差的主要来源，样式对齐可在不改变语义判定的前提下进一步改善性能。

Abstract: We present the first-place solution to the MeViS-Text track of the 8th Large-scale Video Object Segmentation (LSVOS) Challenge 2026: referring video object segmentation guided by written motion expressions, including deceptive no-target expressions that match no object in the video and must yield empty masks in every frame. Our pipeline, SSUPER, resolves each expression into a visual concept, generates full-video candidate masklets with SAM~3.1, and selects target IDs. At every reasoning stage, three heterogeneous multimodal large language models independently execute the same stage-specific prompt before a single synthesis pass commits one schema-validated verdict. Although this system rejects every no-target expression in validation, the leaderboard reveals that a substantial share of test no-target cases still slips through. The reason is that hard negatives name a plausible object and fail only under the complete temporal predicate, so when selection and existence are decided together, a category-plausible masklet anchors the verdict. Hence, we decouple existence verification into an independent multi-agent audit of the full predicate (category, count, action, trajectory, event order, and semantic role) that distinguishes absence from temporary invisibility, discounts apparent motion caused by camera movement, and requires contradicting evidence rather than mere uncertainty for a no-target verdict. Without any new segmentation call, this audit recovers most of the residual no-target errors. A training-data-only StyleRefiner then aligns mask geometry with the annotation style of MeViSv2 while preserving every presence decision by construction, showing that once the semantics are fixed, part of the remaining error is stylistic rather than semantic. The complete system reaches a Final score of 0.9081339614 on the official challenge leaderboard.

</details>


### [12] [Gaussian Meta-Space Augmentation for Stacking Ensembles in Multimodal IPMN Risk Stratification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11472)
*Max A. Nelson,Eminenur Sen Tasci,Zhixiang Wang,Zongwei Zhou,Halil Ertugrul Aktas,Andrea M. Bejar,Elif Keles,Ziliang Hong,Sıtkı Safa Taflan,Muhammed Enes Tasci,Frank H. Miller,Michael B. Wallace,Rajesh N. Keswani,Gorkem Durak,Ulas Bagci*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出cUPMI方法，一种用于IPMN胰腺癌风险分层的集成堆叠组合器正则化技术。通过类条件高斯增强组合器的对数概率元特征，在多中心分析中测试于不同预测范式。结果显示cUPMI对L2逻辑回归堆叠增益有限，但持续正则化高容量树组合器（RF和XGBoost在二分类中分别+0.015和+0.024 AUC），且在XGBoost的8流放射组学三分类任务中达到+0.022 QWK。折叠锁定的放射组学与2.5D CNN流融合的RF堆叠达到最佳整体性能（QWK 0.595，二分类AUC 0.839）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 胰腺癌是最致命的恶性肿瘤之一，IPMN的风险分层对早期干预至关重要，但通常需要侵入性组织活检。基于视觉的方法（放射组学和深度学习）提供了有前景但相互独立的判别机会，而多序列MRI和解剖分解分析提供额外互补信号。有效融合这些信息对于IPMN异型增生风险的序数预测至关重要。

Method: 提出cUPMI方法，即对组合器的对数概率元特征进行类条件高斯增强。将放射组学、2.5D CNN等多种预测流进行集成堆叠，通过精心正则化和校准的组合器进行融合，在多中心数据集上测试不同预测范式（二分类和序数三分类）。

Result: cUPMI在L2逻辑回归二分类堆叠中增益有限，但持续正则化高容量树组合器：RF+0.015和XGBoost+0.024二分类AUC（所有种子均为正增益）。对XGBoost的8流放射组学三分类任务（no<low<high）提升+0.022 QWK。折叠锁定的放射组学与2.5D CNN融合的RF堆叠达到最佳性能：QWK 0.595（95% CI [0.54, 0.64]），二分类AUC 0.839，超越放射组学、2.5D ResNet和3D DenseNet-121基线。

Conclusion: cUPMI作为类条件高斯增强的集成组合器正则化方法，对高容量树组合器（尤其XGBoost）在IPMN风险分层中具有一致的改进效果；放射组学与2.5D CNN特征流的融合堆叠是构建最强整体模型的有效策略。

Abstract: Pancreatic cancer is among the most lethal malignancies; risk stratification of intraductal papillary mucinous neoplasms (IPMNs) offers a crucial opportunity for early intervention but typically requires invasive tissue biopsy. Dominant vision-based approaches, including radiomics and deep learning, provide promising but initially separate discrimination opportunities. Similarly, multisequence MRI (T1W/T2W) and anatomically decomposed (head, body and tail) analysis of the pancreas provide additional and potentially complementary signals. Effective fusion of this information is crucial in ordinal IPMN dysplasia risk prediction and can be accomplished via a meticulously regularized and calibrated ensemble stacking combiner. We present cUPMI, a class-conditional Gaussian augmentation of a combiner's log-probability meta-features, and test it on various prediction paradigms. In our multi-center analysis, we find cUPMI adds limited value to properly regularized L2-logistic binary classification stacks, but consistently regularizes higher-capacity tree combiners in the binary and radiomics-only setting (RF +0.015 and XGBoost +0.024 binary AUC, positive in all seeds). Its cleanest ordinal benefit appears for XGBoost on an 8-stream radiomics task (3-class no < low < high, +0.022 QWK in all seeds). Separately, fold-locked fusion of radiomics and 2.5D CNN streams yields the strongest overall model, an RF stack reaching QWK 0.595 (95% CI [0.54, 0.64]) and binary AUC 0.839, surpassing radiomics, 2.5D ResNet, and 3D DenseNet-121 baselines.

</details>


### [13] [Test-Time Hallucination Control in Large Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11474)
*Mehran Tamjidi,Hamidreza Dastmalchi,Ali Cheraghian,Mohammadreza Alimoradijazi,Aijun An,Hossein Rahmani*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为TTH（Test-Time Hallucination Mitigation）的训练无关方法，通过引入零样本多模态分类器作为令牌验证器，在测试阶段将图像驱动的辅助logits与LVLM原始输出进行令牌级融合，并采用基于熵的加权策略，有效缓解大视觉语言模型中的对象幻觉问题，且不会引入额外的解码开销或破坏预训练知识。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对象幻觉问题严重制约了LVLM在实际应用中的可靠性。现有训练方法性能好但成本高，而训练无关方法要么需要多次解码导致计算开销大，要么以模型特定方式修改内部状态而可能损害预训练知识，亟需一个高效且通用的解决方案。

Method: TTH方法包含三个核心组件：(1) 令牌验证器模块，采用零样本多模态分类器（MMC）生成基于输入图像的辅助logits；(2) 在令牌级别将辅助logits与LVLM原始输出进行融合，仅处理从候选池中选出的对象令牌；(3) 基于熵的加权方案，实现稳健且准确的预测。

Result: 在多个LVLM家族和多样化基准上的大量实验表明，TTH方法持续提升了准确性和鲁棒性，验证了其泛化能力和实际有效性。

Conclusion: TTH作为一种无需训练、无需多次解码的通用方法，能够在不损害预训练知识的前提下有效缓解对象幻觉，具有良好的泛化性和实际应用价值。

Abstract: Object Hallucination in large vision-language models (LVLMs), where models generate non-factual content about input images, remains a critical barrier to their reliability in real-world applications. Existing mitigation strategies can be categorized into training-based and training-free methods. Training-based methods often achieve strong performance but are costly, requiring extensive computational resources, large-scale data, and time-consuming fine-tuning. Training-free approaches are particularly appealing due to their efficiency. However, existing training-free methods either require multiple decoding rounds, which adds computational overhead, or modify internal states in a model-specific way that risks degrading pretrained knowledge. We propose Test-Time Hallucination Mitigation (TTH) method, a novel training-free method that addresses both limitations. TTH introduces a token-validator module, implemented as a zero-shot Multi-Modal Classifier (MMC), to generate auxiliary logits grounded in the input image. These logits are fused with the original LVLM outputs at the token level for object tokens selected from a candidate pool. An entropy-based weighting scheme is then applied to enable robust and accurate predictions. Extensive experiments across multiple LVLM families and diverse benchmarks demonstrate that TTH consistently improves accuracy and robustness, underscoring its generalizability and practical effectiveness. Code is released at https://github.com/Mehran-TAM/TTH

</details>


### [14] [Language-Structured Relational Q-Learning for Threat-Aware Control in Safety-Critical Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11498)
*Aditya Humnabadkar,Huaizhong Zhang,Ardhendu Behera*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出语言结构化关系Q学习（通过Ego-Centric Relational Q-Network实现），在2,500个安全关键驾驶场景中，语言结构化训练将测试成功率从49-52%提升至55-58%，并将对威胁的关注度从1.2倍提高到2.1倍。然而，这种表征上的提升并未持续转化为自适应控制——训练策略的性能与最佳恒定动作相当，而简单策略组合可解决76%的场景。作者将这一差异形式化为"识别-控制鸿沟"，并证明奖励重新加权和边际塑造无法消除由此产生的策略崩溃。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于自然语言的场景生成提供了一种直观描述罕见且复杂驾驶交互的方式，但语言结构化数据训练是否真正产生自适应控制策略仍不确定，本文旨在探究这一问题。

Method: 提出Language-Structured Relational Q-Learning，通过Ego-Centric Relational Q-Network（ERQ-Net）从动态交通图中联合学习车辆间相关性和行动价值。训练时语言描述定义周围车辆行为，但推理时提示和语义角色对策略隐藏，ERQ-Net必须仅从可观测的运动学和交互中推断威胁相关性。

Result: 在2,500个安全关键场景中，语言结构化训练将成功率从49-52%提升至55-58%，威胁注意力从1.2x提升至2.1x；但训练策略与最佳恒定动作表现相当，简单策略组合可解决76%场景。奖励重新加权和边际塑造无法消除策略崩溃。评估涵盖真实性、关键性、语义准确性和向CARLA的迁移。

Conclusion: 语言结构化表征学习能够带来更强的威胁感知能力，但识别威胁并不等于能够自适应控制。研究揭示了"识别-控制鸿沟"这一关键局限，表明单纯的语言结构化训练和奖励调整不足以产生真正的自适应策略，需要进一步研究以弥合表征收益与控制性能之间的差距。

Abstract: Natural-language-based scenario generation offers an intuitive means of describing rare and complex driving interactions, yet it is still uncertain whether training with language-structured data leads to truly adaptive control policies. We propose Language-Structured Relational Q-Learning, instantiated through an Ego-Centric Relational Q-Network (ERQ-Net), which jointly learns inter-vehicle relevance and action values from dynamic traffic graphs. Language descriptions define surrounding-vehicle behaviours during training, while prompts and semantic actor roles are hidden from the policy. ERQ-Net must therefore infer threat relevance solely from observable kinematics and interactions. Across 2,500 safety-critical scenarios, language-structured training improves test success from 49-52% to 55-58% and increases adversary-focused attention from 1.2x to 2.1x, demonstrating emergent threat awareness. However, this representational gain does not consistently translate into adaptive control: trained policies perform similarly to the best constant action, while a portfolio of simple policies solves 76% of scenarios. We formalise this discrepancy as a recognition-control gap and show that reward reweighting and margin shaping do not eliminate the resulting policy collapse. Evaluations of realism, criticality, semantic accuracy, and transfer of state-interface representations to CARLA further highlight both the strengths and the constraints of language-structured relational policy learning in safety-critical driving scenarios.

</details>


### [15] [New Orthogonal Multiwavelet Filters Derived by Matrix Spectral Factorization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11518)
*Vasil Kolev,Todor Cooklev,Fritz Keinert*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文利用Fast Bauer矩阵谱分解方法，基于正交CL多小波滤波器的矩阵乘积滤波器构造了两个新的超紧支撑正交多小波，其中一个是据报道在编码和平滑度方面优于其他超紧支撑多小波。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有超紧支撑多小波在编码效率和平滑度方面存在不足，需要构造性能更优的新型正交多小波以改善信号处理和图像应用的效果。

Method: 采用Fast Bauer方法对正交CL多小波滤波器的矩阵乘积滤波器进行矩阵谱分解，构造两个新的正交多小波，并通过子带边缘检测、图像压缩和信号去噪等实验与GHM、SA4、CL、Integer Haar和Alpert等多滤波器进行对比分析。

Result: 新构造的多小波具有正交性、对称/反对称性，其中一个在编码和平滑度方面优于其他超紧支撑多小波。在图像压缩和去噪应用中，新多小波在人眼视觉度量、SSIM和MS-SSIM指标上表现更好。

Conclusion: 新构造的超紧支撑正交多小波在图像压缩和去噪等应用中具有更好的视觉质量和相似度指标，展现出优于现有基准多滤波器的性能。

Abstract: The paper considers the construction of two new orthogonal multiwavelets with supercompact support by using the Fast Bauer's method for matrix spectral factorization on the matrix product filter of the orthogonal CL multiwavelet filter. The new multiwavelets possess orthogonality, symmetry/antisymmetry, and one of them provides better coding and smoothness than other supercompact multiwavelets.
  The performance of the new multiwavelet filters in subband-based edge detection, grayscale and color image compression and 1D and 2D signal denoising is compared with the GHM, SA4, CL, Integer Haar and Alpert multifilters. The comparative analysis shows that new multiwavelets can provides better human visual measures, SSIM and MS-SSIM in image compression and denoising applications.

</details>


### [16] [Generative Semantic Segmentation via an Observable Semantic-Image Interface and Hierarchical Generator Evidence Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11537)
*Weize Cai,Yongqi Dong,Zhida Shao,Zixin Fu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Semantic Prism，一个条件语义图像生成与细化框架，采用确定性推理。通过扩散蒸馏的一步生成器渲染语义RGB图像，利用像素颜色与固定类颜色码本的逐像素距离定义显式概率接口。层级生成器证据对齐（HGE）在接口logit空间中预测加性残差，保留图像定义的接口作为最终分布的参考。此外，提出的C-IHD方法无需辅助预测器或额外前向传播即可对剩余像素误差进行排序。在Cityscapes验证集上达到72.07% mIoU，比直接接口解码高11.39 mIoU点，预期校准误差为0.41%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式语义分割将结构化预测暴露为图像，但直接颜色解码易受颜色漂移和边界混合影响，而潜在特征解码器预测独立输出分布时可能将渲染图像降级为中间可视化。现有方法缺乏一种既能利用图像生成优势又能保持精确概率接口的统一框架。

Method: Semantic Prism包含三个核心组件：(1) 扩散蒸馏的一步生成器渲染语义RGB图像；(2) 基于渲染颜色到固定类颜色码本的逐像素距离定义显式概率接口；(3) 层级生成器证据对齐（HGE）空间对齐多层级生成器特征，使用零初始化输出投影在接口logit空间中预测加性残差；(4) Contextual Interface-Hierarchy Disagreement (C-IHD)作为固定读出机制，无需辅助预测器即可对剩余像素误差排序。

Result: 在Cityscapes 500张图像验证集上达到72.07% mIoU（比直接接口解码高11.39 mIoU点），预期校准误差0.41%。在BDD100K上达到62.22% mIoU，源冻结迁移到ACDC数据集达到46.89% mIoU。C-IHD在三个数据集上均优于最大softmax概率的像素误差排序性能，在ACDC上将AUPR从0.6580提升到0.7557。

Conclusion: Semantic Prism展示了通过保持图像定义接口作为最终分布参考的方式，可以同时获得生成式语义分割的视觉优势和精确概率输出。确定性推理和零初始化残差预测确保了稳定性，C-IHD提供了一种计算成本极低的可靠不确定性估计方法，在多个数据集上验证了方法的有效性和泛化能力。

Abstract: Generative semantic segmentation exposes structured predictions as images, but direct color decoding is susceptible to color drift and boundary mixing, whereas latent-feature decoders that predict a separate output distribution may relegate the rendered image to an intermediate visualization. We present Semantic Prism, a conditional semantic-image generation-and-refinement framework with deterministic inference. A diffusion-distilled one-step generator renders a semantic RGB image; per-pixel distances from the rendered colors to a fixed class-color codebook define an explicit probabilistic interface. Hierarchical Generator Evidence Alignment spatially aligns multi-level generator features and uses a zero-initialized output projection to predict an additive residual in the interface logit space, retaining the image-defined interface as the reference for the final distribution. The interface and refined distributions further enable Contextual Interface--Hierarchy Disagreement (C-IHD), a fixed readout for ranking remaining pixel errors without an auxiliary predictor or additional forward pass. On the 500-image Cityscapes validation set, Semantic Prism achieves 72.07% mean intersection over union, 11.39 mIoU points above direct-interface decoding, with 0.41% expected calibration error. Matched-capacity ablations over three seeds support the benefit of jointly aligned multi-level evidence. A separately trained model attains 62.22% mIoU on BDD100K, while the Cityscapes-trained model reaches 46.89\% mIoU under source-frozen transfer to the Adverse Conditions Dataset with Correspondences, without target-domain adaptation. Across all three datasets, C-IHD consistently improves the area under the precision--recall curve for pixel-error ranking over maximum softmax probability on the same segmentation predictions; on ACDC, it raises AUPR from 0.6580 to 0.7557.

</details>


### [17] [Through Van Gogh's Eyes: Global Style Transfer with Diffusion Mod](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11546)
*Jeongha Lee,Yujin Kim,Ghazanfar Ali,Suhyun Kim,Jae-In Hwang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Global Style Transfer (GST)范式及Global Style Guidance (GSG)方法，以Many-to-One方式聚合艺术家的多幅作品，在扩散模型的h-space中学习残差全局风格偏移，同时提出Content Alignment Guidance (CAG)保持内容语义结构，从而实现对艺术家整体风格而非单幅作品的风格迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有艺术图像合成方法存在两大局限：传统风格迁移方法仅能进行One-to-One的单幅作品级风格化，无法捕捉艺术家的整体风格分布；基于艺术家名字的文本到图像扩散模型存在文本诱导偏差，仅能重现少数标志性作品的模式。因此需要一种能捕获艺术家全局风格的新范式。

Method: 提出GST范式，采用Many-to-One方式聚合目标艺术家的多幅作品并将其共享的全局风格迁移到单幅内容图像上。核心方法包括两个组件：1) GSG (Global Style Guidance)，在扩散模型的h-space（中间特征空间）中基于固定提示词学习残差全局风格偏移，纯从视觉统计中学习艺术家级风格语义以缓解文本依赖偏差；2) CAG (Content Alignment Guidance)，一种无需训练感知引导机制，在保持内容图像语义结构的同时允许艺术家特定的几何变形。

Result: 在WikiArt数据集上的实验表明，相比现有风格迁移和基于扩散模型的艺术合成方法，GST在风格保真度、内容保持和输出多样性方面均取得了更优的性能。

Conclusion: 通过Many-to-One的全局风格聚合学习，GST有效解决了传统方法仅能捕捉单幅作品风格和扩散模型存在文本偏差的问题，为艺术图像合成提供了一种能够真正捕捉艺术家整体风格分布的新范式。

Abstract: Artistic image synthesis aims to recreate the expressive visual identity of a target artist, yet existing methods often fail to capture an artist's global style. Conventional style transfer methods transfer the style of one or a few reference artworks to a content image in a One-to-One manner, making them effective for artwork-level stylization but limited in representing the broader stylistic distribution of an artist. Text-to-image diffusion models conditioned on artist names, such as '~ in Van Gogh style', offer greater flexibility, but they often suffer from text-induced bias and reproduce patterns from only a few iconic works. To address these limitations, we introduce Global Style Transfer (GST), an artistic image synthesis paradigm, in a Many-to-One manner, that aggregates multiple artworks from a target artist and transfers their shared global style to a single content image. For GST, we propose Global Style Guidance (GSG), which learns a residual global style offset in the intermediate feature space, or h-space, of a diffusion model under a fixed prompt. By learning artist-level style semantics purely from visual statistics, GSG mitigates text-dependent artistic bias. We further propose Content Alignment Guidance (CAG), a training-free perceptual guidance mechanism that preserves the semantic structure of the content image while allowing artist-specific geometric deformation. Experiments on WikiArt demonstrate that GST achieves superior stylistic fidelity, content preservation, and output diversity compared to existing style transfer and diffusion-based artistic synthesis methods.

</details>


### [18] [From Synthesis to Removal: Physics-Grounded Reflection Simulation and Diffusion-Based Video Dereflection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11562)
*Zepeng Wang,Jiagao Hu,Fuhao Li,Yuxuan Chen,Fei Wang,Daiguo Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一个闭环框架S2R，统一了物理驱动的反射仿真、基于扩散模型的视频去反射以及基准评估。包括S2R-Synthesis数据生成管道、S2R-Removal去反射模型和S2R-Bench评估基准，在多个基准上达到SOTA性能且推理速度更快。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频反射去除任务长期被忽视，主要面临三方面挑战：缺乏成对的视频训练数据、缺乏时间一致性去除模型、缺乏专门的评估基准。尽管单图反射去除已被广泛研究，但视频场景下的反射问题仍未得到充分探索。

Method: 提出闭环框架S2R：(1) S2R-Synthesis管道在结构空间进行物理驱动的增强（模拟粗糙引起的模糊、厚度引起的重影和反射率变化），并训练视频扩散渲染器生成逼真的反射视频；(2) S2R-Removal是首个基于扩散的视频反射去除模型，通过反射感知潜在适配和一步像素-几何精炼，在单一去噪步骤中恢复干净透射；(3) S2R-Bench是首个视频反射去除基准，支持全参考评估和真实世界人工感知评估。

Result: 在S2R-Bench和多个公开图像基准上的实验表明，该方法达到最先进性能，且推理速度比非扩散基线更快，验证了S2R-Synthesis的有效性。

Conclusion: 通过数据合成、模型设计和基准评估三个环节的闭环协同，S2R框架有效解决了视频反射去除数据缺乏、模型缺失和评估空白的问题，为该任务确立了新的标准，并展示了扩散模型在该领域的潜力。

Abstract: Videos captured through glass often contain reflections that degrade visual quality and interfere with downstream vision tasks. Although single-image reflection removal has been extensively studied, video reflection removal remains largely underexplored due to the lack of paired video data, temporally coherent removal models, and dedicated evaluation benchmarks. We present a closed-loop framework that unifies physics-grounded reflection simulation, diffusion-based video dereflection, and benchmark evaluation. Our S2R-Synthesis pipeline generates paired reflected and reflection-free videos by performing physics-grounded augmentation in the structure space and rendering realistic reflected videos with a trained video diffusion renderer; the augmentation models key glass-related effects including roughness-induced blur, thickness-induced ghosting, and reflectance variation. Based on the synthesized data, we introduce S2R-Removal, the first diffusion-based video reflection removal model, which adapts a pretrained video diffusion prior through reflection-aware latent adaptation and one-step pixel-geometric refinement, recovering the clean transmission in a single denoising step. We further build S2R-Bench, the first benchmark for video reflection removal, supporting both full-reference evaluation and real-world human perceptual assessment. Experiments on S2R-Bench and multiple public image benchmarks demonstrate state-of-the-art performance and faster inference than even non-diffusion baselines, and validate the effectiveness of S2R-Synthesis. Project page: https://codingwzp.github.io/VideoDereflection_S2R.

</details>


### [19] [Repurposing RGB-based Foundation Model for Depth Estimation on Thermal Images Using Hierarchical Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11564)
*Jie Hong,Tingtian Li,Xuesong Li,Xiao Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出RGB-HS框架，通过从RGB基础模型中提取层次化监督信号来实现热成像图像的深度估计，在多个层级对齐两个编码器的token，并结合验证机制根据RGB图像质量加权，从而提升热图像深度估计的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 热成像深度估计在夜间、雨天等恶劣条件下对机器人应用至关重要。现有的迁移学习方法虽然尝试将RGB基础模型的知识迁移到热成像模态，但未能充分利用这些模型中编码的丰富层次化表征，因此需要更有效的知识迁移方法。

Method: RGB-HS框架包含三个关键组件：(1) 将热成像编码器替换为基础模型，并引入一个使用相同架构基础模型作为编码器的并行RGB分支；(2) 在两个编码器的token之间进行多层级对齐，使热学生分支同时捕获结构精度和语义抽象；(3) 引入验证机制，根据RGB图像质量对RGB分支的token进行加权，以细化对齐过程。

Result: 在公开基准数据集上的大量实验表明，RGB-HS取得了具有竞争力的性能，并且更有效地利用了RGB基础模型的表征能力来完成热图像的深度估计任务。

Conclusion: RGB-HS通过层次化监督和验证机制，成功提升了热成像深度估计的性能，验证了充分利用RGB基础模型层次化表征的有效性。

Abstract: Depth estimation from thermal images is highly valuable for robotic applications in adverse conditions, such as nighttime and rainy weather. Recent studies have sought to transfer knowledge from RGB-based foundation models to thermal modalities, yet the rich hierarchical representations these models encode remain underutilized. To address this limitation, we propose RGB-HS, a novel framework for thermal-image depth estimation that leverages hierarchical supervision from an RGB-based foundation model. Specifically, we first replace the baseline thermal encoder with a foundational model and introduce a parallel RGB branch that also employs a foundational model as an encoder of the same architecture, taking RGB images as input. The alignment is then performed across multiple levels between the tokens of the two encoders, allowing the thermal student branch to capture both structural precision and semantic abstraction from the RGB teacher branch. Furthermore, we introduce verification to refine the alignment process by weighting tokens from the RGB branch based on RGB image quality. Extensive experiments on the popular benchmark demonstrate that RGB-HS achieves competitive performance and more effectively exploits the representational capacity of RGB-based foundation models for depth estimation on thermal images.

</details>


### [20] [Hand Visibility Detector: Per-Keypoint Visibility Estimation for Hands](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11574)
*Ryosei Hara,Masashi Hatano,Rintaro Yanagi,Atsushi Hashimoto,Takuma Yagi,Mariko Isogawa*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文首次将手部关节可见性估计作为独立任务进行系统研究，提出了Hand Visibility Detector模型，利用大规模预训练的HPE模型作为骨干网络来估计每个手部关节的可见性，并在下游3D手部姿态标注任务中通过可见性加权三角化减少了重投影误差。代码和演示已开源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在AR/VR和机器人等应用中，手部关节的可见性对评估遮挡情况下姿态估计结果的可靠性至关重要，但现有HPE方法大多只输出关节位置而不显式指示可见性，且可见性仅作为姿态估计的辅助信号，尚未被作为独立任务系统研究。

Method: 提出Hand Visibility Detector模型，使用在大规模数据上预训练的HPE模型作为骨干网络，利用其先验知识来估计单个手部关节的可见性，并展示了该模型在3D手部姿态标注下游任务中的应用，通过可见性加权三角化方式融合多视角2D关键点。

Result: 利用预训练HPE模型的先验知识作为骨干网络能在此任务中获得高性能表现；在下游的3D手部姿态标注任务中，可见性加权三角化相比未加权方法有效降低了重投影误差。方法已作为即用型软件包发布。

Conclusion: 本文填补了手部关节可见性估计作为独立任务研究的空白，证明了预训练HPE知识迁移的有效性以及可见性信息在3D姿态标注中的实用价值，为相关应用提供了可部署的开源工具。

Abstract: Hand Pose Estimation (HPE) is a fundamental technology for various applications such as AR/VR and robotics. In these applications, the visibility of each hand joint in the image is crucial for assessing the reliability of estimation results under occlusion. However, most existing HPE methods output joint positions without explicitly indicating their visibility. Although some methods account for occlusion or visibility, visibility estimation has mainly been used as an auxiliary signal for improving pose estimation. To our knowledge, per-joint hand visibility estimation has not been systematically studied as a standalone task. In this work, we propose Hand Visibility Detector, a model for estimating the visibility of individual hand joints, and present the first systematic investigation of visibility estimation as an independent task. We show that leveraging the prior knowledge of HPE models pretrained on large-scale data as a backbone yields high performance in this task. We further demonstrate the utility of Hand Visibility Detector on a downstream task of 3D hand pose annotation via multi-view triangulation of 2D keypoints, showing that visibility-weighted triangulation reduces reprojection error. Our method is released as a ready-to-use package, and the code and demo are available at https://github.com/ryhara/hand_visibility_detector .

</details>


### [21] [Early Warning Signals for OpenVLA Failure under Visual Distribution Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29699)
*Dipesh Tharu Mahato,Rachel Ren*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究测试OpenVLA视觉语言动作模型在LIBERO操作任务中，其前馈激活是否包含可线性解码的近期任务失败信息。在遮挡压力测试下（成功率从57%降至17%），第16层逻辑回归探针在15步预测范围内达到AUROC 0.972，优于基线与动作不一致基线。跨层扫描发现可解码性随深度不均匀分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语言动作模型将感知、语言和控制整合于单一策略中，但一旦视觉条件发生偏移，其失败原因难以诊断。现有方法缺乏对模型内部状态进行失败预测的轻量级监控手段，作者希望在控制条件下验证内部激活是否蕴含失败相关信息。

Method: 固定预先训练好的OpenVLA策略，在LIBERO操作rollout中记录执行期间的内部激活。在收集rollout后拟合轻量级逻辑回归监控器。以遮挡为主要受控压力测试，并额外评估颜色偏移（作为良性对照）和相机抖动（作为二次失败诱发条件），不重新拟合模型进行迁移验证。同时进行跨层稀疏扫描比较不同深度的可解码性。

Result: 遮挡条件下，第16层逻辑探针在15步失败预测窗口内达到AUROC 0.972和AUPRC 0.352，优于均值差异方向和动作不一致基线。层扫描显示层16最强，层8仍具信息量，层10较弱。颜色偏移不产生失败（良性对照），相机抖动产生失败且遮挡训练的监控器仍优于随机。

Conclusion: OpenVLA内部状态在受控感知偏移下包含失败相关结构化信息，但实验存在明确局限：未建立因果机制、未验证跨任务泛化能力、也未形成可部署的恢复系统。结果应视为概念验证而非完整解决方案。

Abstract: Vision Language Action models combine perception, language grounding, and control in a single policy, but their failures are hard to diagnose once visual conditions shift. We test whether OpenVLA feedforward activations contain linearly decodable information about near term task failure in LIBERO manipulation rollouts. The policy is fixed throughout. We log internal activations during execution and fit lightweight monitors after the rollouts are collected. Occlusion is the main controlled stress test. It reduces OpenVLA success from $57\%$ to $17\%$ over $100$ episodes per condition. Under this shift, a logistic probe at layer 16 reaches AUROC $0.972$ and AUPRC $0.352$ for predicting failure within a $15$ step horizon. It outperforms both a mean difference direction and an action disagreement baseline. A sparse layer sweep finds uneven decodability across depth: layer 16 is strongest among the tested layers, layer 8 remains informative, and layer 10 is weaker. To check whether the monitor is just an occlusion detector, we also evaluate color shift and camera jitter without refitting. Color shift produces no failures in this setting, so it is a benign control rather than a failure benchmark. Camera jitter does induce failures, and the occlusion trained monitor remains above random. The result is deliberately limited: OpenVLA internal states contain failure relevant structure under controlled perceptual shift, but these experiments do not establish a causal mechanism, task held out generalization, or a deployable recovery system.

</details>


### [22] [A Hybrid Framework of Vision Transformer and Gated Recurrent Unit for Detection of Mosquito Diseases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11582)
*Danial Sharifrazi,Saadat Behzadi,Nouman Javed,Roohallah Alizadehsani,Prasad N. Paradkar,Asim Bhatti*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究提出一个三步框架（YOLOv11M目标检测+ViT特征提取+ConvGRU分类），用于区分登革热病毒感染蚊虫与对照蚊虫，在复杂环境视频中达到88.88%的准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 识别登革热病毒感染蚊虫与对照蚊虫是蚊虫运动行为分析中的重大挑战，因为蚊虫体型小且视频背景复杂，传统AI方法难以从视频帧中提取准确特征，容易产生错误特征。

Method: 提出三步框架：首先使用YOLO 11M模型识别蚊虫并去除背景，然后利用Vision Transformer（ViT）提取视觉特征，最后使用卷积门控循环单元（ConvGRU）分类器对视频进行分类。并对RNN、LSTM、GRU及其卷积版本进行了对比分析。

Result: ConvGRU模型表现最佳，达到88.88%的准确率、84.45%的精确率、82.82%的召回率和82.81%的F1分数。

Conclusion: 将卷积模型与序列网络结合（尤其是ConvGRU）能够同时提取精确的空间特征和长期时间依赖关系，为复杂环境中蚊虫行为分析提供了可靠的解决方案。

Abstract: Identifying dengue virus-infected mosquitoes from control mosquitoes is a major challenge in analyzing mosquito locomotion behavior due to the small size and complexity of the video background. Conventional AI methods are often unable to extract accurate features from video frames and produce erroneous features. In this study, a three-step framework is introduced: first, mosquitoes are identified and the background is removed using the YOLO 11M model, then visual features are extracted using the Vision Transformer (ViT), and finally the videos are classified with a convolutional GRU (ConvGRU) classifier. A comparative analysis of different models, including Recurrent Neural Network (RNN), Long Short-Term Memory (LSTM), Gated Recurrent Unit (GRU), and their convolutional versions showed that the ConvGRU model achieved the best performance; it achieved 88.88% accuracy, 84.45% precision, 82.82% recall, and 82.81% F1 score. These results demonstrate that combining convolutional models with sequence-based networks, especially in the ConvGRU model, allows the simultaneous extraction of precise spatial features and long-term temporal dependencies from mosquito movements. Finally, the proposed framework provides a reliable solution for analyzing mosquito behavior in complex environments.

</details>


### [23] [ProtoHGF-Net: Prototype HyperGraph Fusion with Intra-modal Calibration for RGBT Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11595)
*Xiangqi Chen,Xiuling Zhang,Chengzhuan Yang,Li Zhao,Dawei Zhang,Yanchao Wang,Liyuan Chen,Hua Wang,Hao Peng,Zhonglong Zheng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出ProtoHGF-Net，将RGBT跨模态融合重新定义为原型级语义交互而非密集跨模态交互，通过原型超图融合和教师掩码校准蒸馏，在DroneVehicle、DVTOD和FLIR数据集上达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RGBT目标检测方法依赖全分辨率特征的密集跨模态交互，容易引入背景干扰，阻碍目标相关表征的学习。

Method: 1) 设计原型超图融合（Prototype HyperGraph Fusion），在紧凑的原型级语义空间中进行跨模态交互，实现目标相关原型的选择性融合；2) 提出教师掩码校准蒸馏（Teacher-Mask Calibration Distillation），利用模态特定教师和目标感知掩码在融合前校准模态特征，抑制背景主导响应。

Result: 在DroneVehicle、DVTOD和FLIR数据集上分别达到85.9%、88.2%和79.1%的mAP50，均实现最先进性能。

Conclusion: 原型级融合范式能有效替代密集交互，提升RGBT检测精度并减少背景干扰，代码已开源。

Abstract: RGB-Thermal (RGBT) object detection enables robust perception in complex scenes by leveraging the complementary strengths of visible textures and thermal cues. However, existing methods mainly rely on dense cross-modal interactions over full-resolution features, which inevitably introduce background interference and hinder the learning of target-relevant representations. In this paper, we propose the Prototype HyperGraph Fusion Network (ProtoHGF-Net), a novel framework that redefines cross-modal fusion as prototype-level semantic interaction rather than the dense cross-modal interaction paradigm. Specifically, we design Prototype HyperGraph Fusion to perform cross-modal interaction in a compact prototype-level semantic space. This design enables more selective fusion among target-relevant prototypes. To support this prototype-level fusion, we propose Teacher-Mask Calibration Distillation, which calibrates modality features before fusion using modality-specific teachers and target-aware masks. This strategy suppresses backgrou- nd-dominant responses and produces more target-focused features. Extensive experiments on DroneVehicle, DVTOD, and FLIR demonstrate that ProtoHGF-Net achieves state-of-the-art performance with 85.9\% $mAP_{50}$, 88.2\% $mAP_{50}$, and 79.1\% $mAP_{50}$, respectively. Our code is available at \href{https://github.com/ZiMo-Chen/ProtoHGF}{GitHub}.

</details>


### [24] [How Can Driving World Models Do Counterfactual Prediction?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11601)
*Jiaru Zhang,Can Cui,Yi Xu,Xin Ye,Ruqi Zhang,Ziran Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文揭示了驾驶世界模型在反事实模拟中的根本缺陷：直接基于动作条件的预测未利用事实延续信息，导致无法保真复现实际发生的事件。作者通过构建受控模拟基准验证了这一差距，并提出一种无需训练的简单流水线，将观测证据迁移到反事实视角，显著提升了反事实恢复质量和感知一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 驾驶世界模型通常被用作给定驾驶日志的反事实模拟器，但研究者发现直接的动作条件预测与反事实目标之间存在根本性不匹配：直接预测仅使用共享历史和替代动作，却忽略了历史之后实际发生的事实延续，因此可能生成看似合理但未保留真实事件的未来。

Method: 1) 利用因果框架（abduction-action-prediction）形式化该差距；2) 在短时间范围内（替代动作不改变周围车演化的场景）构建受控模拟基准，包含事实结果和匹配的反事实结果；3) 在两个代表性世界模型上评估直接预测与反事实真值的差距；4) 提出一个训练无关的简单流水线，将观测证据移动到反事实视角，并用冻结模型补全未知部分。

Result: 两个代表性世界模型上的直接预测均未能匹配反事实真值，验证了理论分析。所提出的简单流水线在无需训练的情况下显著提高了恢复比例，并在两个模型上降低了与匹配反事实之间的感知距离。

Conclusion: 这项工作指出了驾驶世界模型在反事实预测中的关键缺口，验证了其可测量性，并展示了即使简单的证据迁移方法也能显著改善反事实恢复效果，呼吁学界关注并推进更优的反事实预测方法。

Abstract: Driving world models are often interpreted as counterfactual simulators for observed driving episodes: given a factual driving log, they are asked what would have happened under an alternative ego action. In this paper, we identify a fundamental mismatch between this goal and direct action-conditioned prediction. The direct prediction uses the shared history and the alternative action but not the factual continuation observed after that history. It can therefore generate a plausible future without preserving what actually happened in this episode. We formalize this gap using the causal recipe of abduction, action, and prediction and study it in a setting with a short time horizon, where the alternative ego action does not alter how surrounding agents evolve. To make the gap measurable, we construct a controlled simulation benchmark with factual outcomes and matched counterfactual outcomes. Across two representative world models, direct predictions fail to match the counterfactual ground truth, supporting our analysis. As a constructive check of this analysis, we introduce a deliberately simple, training-free pipeline that moves observed evidence into the counterfactual view and lets the frozen model complete what remains unknown. Even this simple construction raises the overall recovered fraction substantially and reduces perceptual distance to the matched counterfactual on both models. We hope this work draws attention to this gap and motivates better counterfactual prediction methods for driving world models.

</details>


### [25] [Topology-Aware Query Selection for Surgical Instrument Instance Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11607)
*Ze Zhang,Yang Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出拓扑感知的查询选择方法，将固定Mask2Former的非空候选构建为完全图，学习关系型候选与候选对表示，预测集合基数并求解精确的结构化子集问题，以纠正重复、碎片化、合并、遗漏或空帧等实例集错误。在22例源测试集上，实例F1提升0.0504~0.0612，正帧集合失败率降低0.0848~0.1060；ROBUST-MIPS直接迁移完全复现结果，但Endoscapes仅支持三粒种子中的一粒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高精度的前景掩码仍可能产生错误的手术器械实例集合（重复、碎片化、合并、遗漏或空帧），这些错误在保持良好像素重叠的同时违反对象身份和计数。因此，最终查询选择是一个关系型的、可变基数的结构化问题，而非独立候选决策的集合。

Method: 将固定Mask2Former的非空候选表示为完全图，学习关系型候选表示和候选对表示，预测集合基数，并求解精确的结构化子集问题。形式化比较为完整关系路径与节点特征匹配路径之间的对比，综合评估成对几何、消息传递及额外关系路径容量的组合效应。

Result: 在密封的22例源测试集上，三个发现种子的实例F1均提升0.0504~0.0612，正帧集合失败率降低0.0848~0.1060，且保持了分割保真度和预定义技术安全性。ROBUST-MIPS直接迁移在三个种子中完全复现结果，但Endoscapes仅支持三个种子中的一个。

Conclusion: 所评估的完整关系路径在指定的原生实例契约下，改善了从固定Mask2Former候选构建连贯实例集的效果，但稳定的跨域迁移和组件特定效应尚未得到确立，结论是有界的。

Abstract: Accurate foreground masks can still form an incorrect surgical-instrument instance set: duplicate, fragmented, merged, missed, or empty-frame predictions may preserve favorable pixel overlap while violating object identity and count. Final query selection is therefore a relational, variable-cardinality problem rather than a collection of independent candidate decisions. We evaluate topology-aware query selection, which represents the nonempty candidates of a fixed Mask2Former as a complete graph, learns relational candidate and pair representations, predicts set cardinality, and solves an exact structured subset problem. The formal comparison is the complete relational path versus a node-feature-matched path; it evaluates the combined effect of pairwise geometry, message passing, and the additional relational-path capacity, not an isolated component. On the sealed 22-case source test, all three discovery seeds supported instance-set performance improvement with segmentation fidelity and predefined technical-safety preservation: instance F1 increased by 0.0504--0.0612 and positive-frame set-failure rate decreased by 0.0848--0.1060. Direct ROBUST-MIPS transfer reproduced the complete result in all three seeds. Endoscapes supported only one of three seeds and therefore did not establish stable direct transfer. Taken together, the results support a bounded conclusion: the evaluated complete path improved coherent instance-set construction from fixed Mask2Former candidates in specified native-instance contracts, while stable cross-domain transfer and component-specific effects remain unestablished.

</details>


### [26] [KANResDiff: Learning Local Residual Diffusion via Kolmogorov-Arnold Network for Ambiguous Medical Image Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11617)
*Fanding Li,Chenglin Wang,Xiangyu Li,Xingyu Qiu,Xinghua Ma,Xiangming Yin,Haiyang Li,Suyu Dong,Wei Wang,Kuanquan Wang,Gongning Luo,Shuo Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出KANResDiff，一种基于Kolmogorov-Arnold网络学习局部残差扩散的模糊医学图像分割方法。通过独立时间编码和残差薛定谔桥，在不同推理阶段分配渐进语义角色，实现灵活的动态-随机交互和阶段感知的模糊建模。在两个公开数据集上达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模糊医学图像分割方法以固定且预定义的方式引入随机性，缺乏渐进式语义建模过程，无法为不同阶段分配差异化角色，导致模糊建模能力受限。

Method: (1) 提出独立时间编码（Independent Time Encoding），采用基于样条的时间嵌入替代MLP线性嵌入，增强推理阶段间的独立性并为不同阶段分配渐进语义角色；(2) 提出残差薛定谔桥（Residual Schrodinger Bridge），通过构建局部薛定谔桥注入带有可学习权重的确定性残差先验，替代手工设置，实现灵活的动态-随机交互和阶段感知的模糊建模。

Result: 在两个公开数据集上，KANResDiff在GED指标上最大提升16.8%，在HM-IoU指标上最大提升7.7%，达到SOTA性能，同时MDM指标保持竞争力。

Conclusion: KANResDiff通过KAN学习局部残差扩散，有效解决了现有方法中随机性引入方式的固定性和缺乏渐进语义建模的问题，在模糊医学图像分割任务上取得了显著性能提升。

Abstract: Ambiguous medical image segmentation aims to provide a series of diverse but plausible segmentation hypotheses. However, existing methods introduce stochasticity in a fixed and pre-defined manner, failing to form a progressive semantic modeling process. To address these challenges, we propose KANResDiff to learn local residual diffusion with Kolmogorov-Arnold Network, thereby assigning distinct roles across stages for ambiguity modeling. Specifically, we propose Independent Time Encoding that offers spline-based time embeddings instead of linear ones from MLPs, which enhances the independence across inference stages and assigns progressive semantic roles to different stages. We propose Residual Schrodinger Bridge that injects deterministic residual prior with learnable weights by constructing local Schrodinger Bridge instead of following manually settings, achieving a flexible deterministic-stochastic interaction and stage-aware ambiguity modeling thanks to local optimal diffusion path. Extensive experimental results on two public datasets demonstrate that KANResDiff achieves SOTA performance on GED and HM-IoU, with maximum improvements of 16.8% and 7.7%, respectively, while maintaining competitive performance on the MDM metric. Source code is available at https://github.com/PerceptionComputingLab/KANResDiff.

</details>


### [27] [Generative Video Compression Based on Hierarchical Referencing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11618)
*Daowen Li,Ding Ding,Zifu Zhang,Kai Li,Ying Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出GVCHR（基于层级引用的生成式视频压缩方法），通过分层组织潜在帧，将高质量参考帧同时用于潜在编码和生成重建，在多个基准上相比SOTA方法实现了50.5%和54.0%的BD-rate增益（LPIPS和DISTS指标），显著提升视觉质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散式生成视频压缩方法既未在设计潜在编码时精心考虑参考和质量结构，也未考虑帧级质量变化对去噪过程的影响，这限制了编码效率并加重了生成重建过程中的伪影传播。

Method: GVCHR的核心思想是分层组织潜在帧。在潜在编码方面，将分层参考结构与分层质量结构耦合，为下层Frame分配更多比特（因其被更频繁地用作参考），并引入Hierarchical Temporal Context Mining来挖掘互补的短期和长期时间上下文。在生成重建方面，将编码端层级结构融入Hierarchical Attentive Adapter，该适配器附加在视频扩散Transformer上，通过分层注意力限制每个潜在帧只关注同层或更低层的参考，从而减少去噪过程中的伪影传播。

Result: 在多个基准上验证GVCHR，相比之前的SOTA方法，GVCHR在LPIPS和DISTS指标上分别实现了50.5%和54.0%的BD-rate增益，同时提供了明显改善的视觉质量。

Conclusion: GVCHR通过层级引用策略有效地将编码端与生成端统一在同一分层框架下，兼顾了编码效率和生成重建质量，验证了分层引用在生成式视频压缩中的有效性。

Abstract: Diffusion-based generative video compression has emerged as a promising paradigm to improve perceptual quality, where latent frames are required to be encoded efficiently while serving as denoising conditions. However, existing methods neither carefully design reference and quality structures during latent coding nor account for the impact of frame-level quality variation on denoising procedure, which limits coding efficiency and aggravates artifact propagation during generative reconstruction. In this paper, we propose GVCHR, Generative Video Compression based on Hierarchical Referencing. The key idea is to organize latent frames hierarchically, where the selected high-quality references benefit both latent coding and generative reconstruction. In latent coding, GVCHR couples a hierarchical reference structure with a hierarchical quality structure, assigning more bits to lower-layer frames that are reused more frequently as references. Built on this design, we introduce Hierarchical Temporal Context Mining to exploits complementary short- and long-term temporal context for effective latent coding. In generative reconstruction, the coding-side hierarchy is incorporated into a Hierarchical Attentive Adapter which is attached to a video diffusion transformer. This adapter uses hierarchical attention to restrict each latent frame to attend only to the same- or lower-layer references, thereby reducing artifact propagation during denoising. Experiments validate GVCHR on multiple benchmarks. Compared with the previous state-of-the-art method, GVCHR achieves 50.5% and 54.0% BD-rate gains in terms of LPIPS and DISTS, respectively, while also delivering clearly improved visual quality.

</details>


### [28] [CAM-Guided Saliency Cutout and Image-Based Malware Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11634)
*Yasaman Ebrahimi,Martin Jurecek,Mark Stamp*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究了将HiResCAM显著性引导的cutout数据增强应用于CNN训练的效果，对比了无cutout、随机cutout、低显著性cutout和高显著性cutout四种条件。实验在恶意软件图像数据集RawMal-TF和自然图像数据集CIFAR-100上进行，使用ResNet18模型。结果显示，在恶意软件图像上所有cutout方案都略微降低性能，而在CIFAR-100上低显著性cutout略有提升，表明显著性引导的cutout效果具有领域依赖性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Cutout作为一种数据增强方法可减少过拟合，但其位置的放置方式是否可以通过显著性引导来优化尚未得到充分验证。作者希望测试能否利用HiResCAM改进cutout的放置位置，并探究该方法在恶意软件图像与自然图像上的适用性差异。

Method: 采用ResNet18模型，在RawMal-TF恶意软件图像数据集（17个家族，每家族约1000样本）和CIFAR-100自然图像数据集上进行实验。设置四种训练条件：无cutout、标准随机cutout、低显著性cutout（去除低显著性区域）、高显著性cutout（去除高显著性区域）。测试cutout面积比例为5%、10%、20%、30%，每张训练图像生成M∈{4,8}个增强副本，训练约100个epoch。

Result: 在RawMal-TF恶意软件图像数据集上，三种cutout方案（随机、高显著性、低显著性）均比无cutout的表现略微更差。而在CIFAR-100数据集上，低显著性cutout有轻微的性能提升。总体而言，cutout在不同数据集上的效果不一致。

Conclusion: 显著性引导的cutout的价值具有领域依赖性。恶意软件图像不应被等同于自然图像来处理，在恶意软件图像分类中应用cutout需要谨慎考虑，简单的显著性引导策略可能并不适用。

Abstract: Dropout regularization is commonly used to reduce overfitting by removing parts of a neural network during training. For Convolutional Neural Networks (CNN), cutouts serve a somewhat analogous purpose. Cutouts can be implemented as data augmentation: the original training image is retained, and additional copies are created with regions removed. In this chapter, we test whether cutout placement can be improved by using High-Resolution Class Activation Mapping (HiResCAM). We compare four controlled training conditions: no cutout, standard random cutout, low-saliency cutout, and high-saliency cutout. We experiment using grayscale malware images from the RawMal-TF dataset (17 families with~1,000 samples per family), and for comparison to natural images, we experiment with the well-known CIFAR-100 dataset. All experiments are based on ResNet18 with~100 training epochs. For the cutout experiments, we test cutout areas of~5\%, 10\%, 20\%, and~30\%, and we consider~$M\in\{4,8}$ augmented copies per original training image. The RawMal-TF results are slightly worse for all three cutout cases (random, high and low saliency) as compared to no cutouts. In contrast, our CIFAR-100 experimental results improve slightly under low-saliency cutout. These results suggest that the value of saliency-guided cutout is domain dependent, and that malware images should not be treated as equivalent to natural images.

</details>


### [29] [Robustness of AI-Art Detectors under Generator Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11643)
*Shivank Singh Thakur,Meien Li,Mark Stamp*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究基于SD3.5 Medium艺术数据集，测试了五个AI艺术检测器在跨生成器（U-Net到扩散Transformer）场景下的表现，发现深度学习模型在分布内表现良好但在生成器迁移时严重退化，其中CLIP ViT-L/14表现最佳，但整体存在明显的泛化差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散Transformer等新型生成架构迅速发展，AI生成图像与人类艺术难以区分，而现有艺术检测器大多在同一生成器家族内训练和评估，对更新架构的鲁棒性缺乏探索，这涉及版权保护、虚假信息、欺诈等重大安全问题。

Method: 构建包含十种艺术风格的Stable Diffusion 3.5 Medium艺术作品数据集，通过对保留的人类艺术作品样本进行反向提示生成。训练五个检测器于基于U-Net的潜在扩散艺术作品上，并在SD3.5m数据集上进行零样本跨生成器评估。使用Grad-CAM分析进行可视化解释。

Result: 深度学习检测器在分布内表现强劲，但面临生成器迁移时性能退化，将许多SD3.5m图像误分类为人类作品，而人类假阳性率保持较低。CLIP ViT-L/14模型整体表现最佳，Grad-CAM分析显示假阴性样本上的激活更弱且更分散。

Conclusion: 当前AI艺术检测器存在显著的泛化差距，无法可靠应对快速演进的生成架构。研究强调检测器应作为分层防御体系的一个组成部分，而非独立解决方案，以保持跨架构的可靠性。

Abstract: Text-to-image generative models have advanced rapidly, with modern Diffusion Transformer architectures producing images that are increasingly difficult to distinguish from human-created artwork. This development has raised significant concerns regarding copyright protection, misinformation, fraud, impersonation, and the authenticity of digital content. Most AI-art detectors are trained and evaluated on the same generator family, leaving robustness to newer architectures underexplored. In this chapter, we analyze generator shift based on a Stable Diffusion 3.5 Medium (SD3.5m) artwork dataset spanning ten art styles through reverse prompting of held-out human artwork samples. Five detectors are trained on U-Net-based latent diffusion artwork and evaluated in a zero-shot cross-generator setting on the SD3.5m dataset. Deep learning models perform strongly in-distribution but degrade under generator shift, misclassifying many SD3.5m images as human while human false positives remain low. The CLIP ViT-L/14 model performs best overall, while Grad-CAM analysis reveals weaker and more diffuse activation on false negatives. These findings highlight a generalization gap in current AI-art detectors and motivate the development of detectors as one component of a layered defense that remains reliable across rapidly evolving generative architectures.

</details>


### [30] [Cloak of Invisibility: Real-Time Privacy-Preserving Volumetric Video Streaming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11645)
*Hossein Khalili,Philip Do,Alexander Vilesov,Kittipat Apicharttrisorn,Nader Sehatbakhsh*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出InViStream，一个面向体积视频流的实时"源头隐私保护"系统，解决多视角RGB-D融合场景下私人内容移除问题。系统结合物体检测与深度感知掩蔽，在所有校准视角间传播公共/私人决策，仅融合经过消毒的点云，在保持实时流传输（30 FPS以上）的同时有效保护隐私。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 体积视频流将隐私问题从二维扩展到三维多视角场景。普通视频可以逐帧遮盖敏感内容，但RGB-D体积管线从多个相机捕捉人物、房间和个人物品并融合为共享3D表示，任何视角遗漏或部分移除的私人对象都可能重新出现在重建场景中。现有体积流系统主要优化重建、数据传输和延迟，而隐私保护视觉方法仅针对单相机单帧图像，不适用于校准的多视图RGB-D融合。因此需要一种从源头保护隐私的实时系统。

Method: InViStream通过三种机制解决体积捕捉中的隐私挑战：（1）结合物体检测与深度感知掩蔽，处理物体在不同视角表现差异及RGB掩蔽导致深度几何泄露的问题；（2）在所有校准视角间传播公共/私人决策，确保同一类别实例被一致地分类；（3）仅融合经过消毒的点云，防止云侧融合过程中私人内容在3D重建中重现。

Result: 在包含办公室、会议室、客厅以及多个公共/私人人物和物体的合成及真实RGB-D场景上评估，InViStream在合成数据上达到Dice/Recall为0.799/0.891，在真实数据上达到Dice/Recall为0.792/0.908；合成SSIM高于0.98，且能实现30 FPS以上的实时流传输。

Conclusion: InViStream有效解决了体积视频流中的多视角隐私保护问题，通过在源头（相机端）移除私人内容的同时保持公共场景的重建质量，实现了隐私保护与实时性能的兼顾，为3D远程呈现、教育、娱乐和沉浸式应用提供了可行的隐私保护方案。

Abstract: Volumetric video streaming turns privacy into a 3D, multi-view problem. Unlike ordinary video, where sensitive content can often be redacted frame by frame, RGB-D volumetric pipelines capture people, rooms, and personal objects from multiple cameras and fuse them into a shared 3D representation. A private object missed in one view, or only partially removed before fusion, can therefore reappear in the reconstructed scene. This creates a privacy challenge for 3D telepresence, education, entertainment, and immersive applications: private content should be removed before raw visual and geometric data leave the camera side, while the public part of the scene should remain useful for real-time reconstruction. Existing volumetric streaming systems mainly optimize reconstruction, data movement, and latency, while privacy-preserving vision methods are designed for single-camera, single-frame images and do not directly address calibrated multi-view RGB-D fusion. We present InViStream, a real-time "privacy-from-source" system designed for this setting. InViStream addresses three challenges in volumetric capture: private objects may appear differently across views, RGB masking alone can leave geometric privacy leakage in depth, and public/private instances of the same class must be separated consistently before cloud-side fusion. To address these challenges, InViStream combines object detection with depth-aware masking, propagates public/private decisions across calibrated views, and fuses only sanitized point clouds. We evaluate InViStream on synthetic and real RGB-D scenes, including offices, conference rooms, living rooms, and settings with multiple public and private people and objects. InViStream achieves synthetic Dice/Recall of 0.799/0.891 and real Dice/Recall of 0.792/0.908, with synthetic SSIM above 0.98 and real-time streaming above 30 FPS.

</details>


### [31] [Hybrid-LUT: Channel-Aware Hybrid Lookup Table and Filtering for Efficient Image Denoising](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11646)
*Zhilin Ai,Boyu Li,Sidi Yang,Wenqing Shi,Wenyong Zhou,Binxiao Huang,Chenchen Ding,Ngai Wong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Hybrid-LUT，一种基于YUV色彩空间的非对称通道处理框架，结合查找表和轻量级滤波进行图像去噪。Y通道使用多频带LUT分支恢复精细纹理，UV通道使用轻量级滤波保持色彩一致性，相比RGB-LUT方法存储减少三分之二，仅需421 KB存储即可达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RGB-LUT去噪方法需要三个相同的LUT并行处理RGB通道，导致片上SRAM消耗过大。简单地将LUT处理仅应用于Y通道会产生色彩失真和残余伪影，因为忽略了UV通道。因此需要一种既能降低存储开销又能保持恢复质量的方法。

Method: 提出Hybrid-LUT框架：(1) 基于YUV色彩空间进行非对称通道处理；(2) Y通道采用多频带LUT分支并引入像素级权重融合来恢复精细纹理；(3) UV通道采用轻量级滤波保持色彩一致性；(4) 将LUT与滤波统一设计在同一框架中。

Result: 与RGB-LUT方法相比，LUT存储减少三分之二且保持相同运行时吞吐量。在多个基准数据集上取得SOTA性能，仅需421 KB存储。在真实世界数据集上，相比现有LUT去噪方法CPSNR至少提升0.63 dB。

Conclusion: Hybrid-LUT通过YUV空间的非对称通道处理策略，在显著降低LUT存储开销的同时实现优异去噪性能，验证了其在资源受限边缘设备上进行图像去噪的有效性。

Abstract: Lookup table (LUT)-based image denoising methods have attracted increasing attention due to their high efficiency and hardware-friendly properties. However, existing RGB-LUT approaches require three identical LUTs to process RGB channels in parallel, resulting in large on-chip SRAM consumption. A simple alternative is to apply LUT processing only to the luminance (Y) channel in the YUV color space to reduce memory usage. However, this naive strategy leads to degraded restoration quality, since ignoring the chrominance (UV) channels introduces color distortion and residual artifacts. In this work, we propose Hybrid-LUT, a YUV-based asymmetric channel-processing framework that combines LUT and filtering in a unified design. Specifically, a multi-band LUT branch with pixel-level weight fusion is applied to the Y channel to recover fine textures, while lightweight filtering is used for the UV channels to maintain color consistency. This design reduces LUT storage by two-thirds compared with RGB-LUT methods while maintaining the same runtime throughput. Extensive experiments show that Hybrid-LUT achieves state-of-the-art (SOTA) performance across multiple benchmarks with only 421 KB of storage. In particular, our method surpasses existing LUT-based denoising approaches by at least 0.63 dB CPSNR on real-world datasets, demonstrating its effectiveness for image denoising on resource-constrained edge devices. The project is available at https://github.com/Ai-ZL/Hybrid-LUT .

</details>


### [32] [Motion-as-Prompt: Enhancing Motion Reasoning in Multimodal Large Language Models via Motion-Guided Cross-Frame Visual Prompting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11655)
*Xikai Sun,Kebin Liu,Haotian Wang,Li Liu,Xu Wang,Yunhao Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Motion-as-Prompt (MaP)框架，通过恢复密集点轨迹并直接将轨迹可视化标记在视频帧上，使冻结的多模态大语言模型能够感知帧间隐藏的运动信息，无需训练或修改架构即可显著提升运动中心推理准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型通常采用稀疏均匀采样处理视频以控制计算成本，但这会丢弃采样帧之间的关键过渡信息，限制了模型对物体运动、碰撞和因果交互的推理能力。

Method: MaP框架包含三个步骤：恢复密集点轨迹、选择运动信息丰富的帧、将连续采样帧之间累积的轨迹直接标记到视觉输入上，使隐藏的位移、方向变化和交互对冻结的MLLMs可见。

Result: 在CLEVRER和Something-Something-v2数据集上，MaP分别使GPT-5.5的运动推理准确率提升4.2%和8.9%，且不损害非运动理解能力。

Conclusion: MaP提供了一种简单有效的解决方案，无需模型训练或架构修改即可增强运动中心的视频推理能力，同时保持非运动理解的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Motion-centric video reasoning is fundamental to interactive applications such as robotic manipulation and autonomous navigation. However, multimodal large language models (MLLMs) typically process videos through sparse uniform sampling to control visual-token and attention costs. This strategy may discard critical transitions between sampled frames, limiting reasoning about object movement, collisions, and causal interactions. To mitigate this issue, we propose Motion-as-Prompt (MaP), a track-guided cross-frame visual prompting framework. MaP recovers dense point trajectories, selects motion-informative frames, and marks the trajectories accumulated between consecutive sampled frames directly onto the visual inputs, making otherwise hidden displacement, direction changes, and interactions observable to frozen MLLMs. Experiments on CLEVRER and Something-Something-v2 show that MaP consistently improves average motion-reasoning accuracy, yielding gains of 4.2% and 8.9% for GPT-5.5, respectively. Notably, these improvements are obtained without degrading non-motion understanding, highlighting the robustness of MaP. These results demonstrate that MaP provides a simple and effective solution for enhancing motion-centric video reasoning without model training or architectural modification. Project page:https://github.com/SunVictor23/MaP.

</details>


### [33] [Zero-OVCD: Bridging Training-Free Foundation Models and Pseudo-Label Learning for Open-Vocabulary Change Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11663)
*Daifeng Peng,Yuanke Peng,Haiyan Guan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Zero-OVCD，一个两阶段的开放词汇变化检测（OVCD）框架，无需目标域像素级标注。第一阶段通过互补候选掩膜精炼、多尺度语义相似度融合与响应引导的掩膜校正生成高质量变化伪标签；第二阶段利用伪标签训练变化检测器，并通过检查点投票和高一致性样本选择缓解残留噪声。在四个基准数据集上取得了显著性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的无训练（training-free）开放词汇变化检测管道存在三个关键问题：不准确的候选掩膜、模糊的语义分配以及累积的推理误差，导致检测性能受限。

Method: 提出两阶段框架Zero-OVCD。第一阶段生成高质量变化伪标签：包含互补候选掩膜精炼、基于边际可靠性滤波的多尺度语义相似度融合、以及响应引导的掩膜校正与补全。第二阶段使用伪标签训练变化检测器，引入检查点投票和高一致性样本选择来抑制残余伪标签噪声。

Result: 在LEVIR-CD、WHU-CD和S2Looking上，第一阶段F1分数分别为86.25%、85.82%和50.48%，第二阶段进一步提升至88.65%、88.85%和57.96%。在SECOND上，六类一对多任务的平均宏F1从47.91%提升到50.92%。

Conclusion: 将无训练基础模型推理与噪声感知的伪标签学习相结合，为无需目标域像素级标注的开放词汇变化检测提供了一种有效解决方案。

Abstract: Open-vocabulary change detection (OVCD) enables the identification of user-specified land-cover changes in bitemporal remote sensing images, but existing training-free pipelines remain vulnerable to inaccurate candidate masks, ambiguous semantic assignments, and accumulated inference errors. To address these issues, we propose Zero-OVCD, a two-stage framework that requires no pixel-level annotations from the target domain. In the first stage, high-quality change pseudo-labels are generated through complementary candidate-mask refinement, multiscale semantic similarity fusion with margin-based reliability filtering, and response-guided mask correction and completion. These components jointly suppress noisy candidates, enhance mask-level semantic discrimination, and recover missed change regions. In the second stage, a change detector is trained using the generated pseudo-labels, while checkpoint voting and high-agreement sample selection are introduced to mitigate residual pseudo-label noise. On LEVIR-CD, WHU-CD, and S2Looking, Stage I achieves F1 scores of 86.25%, 85.82%, and 50.48%, while Stage II further improves them to 88.65%, 88.85%, and 57.96%, respectively. On SECOND, the macro-average F1 across six category-wise one-vs-rest tasks increases from 47.91% to 50.92%. These results demonstrate that bridging training-free foundation-model inference with noise-aware pseudo-label learning provides an effective solution for open-vocabulary change detection without target-domain pixel-level annotations. Code will be available at https://github.com/1321663019/Zero-OVCD.

</details>


### [34] [Learning from Multimodal Pseudo-Labels for Robust Open-Vocabulary Instance and Panoptic Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11681)
*Duy Tran Thanh,Yeejin Lee,Byeongkeun Kang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种多模态框架，利用预训练视觉-语言模型（Grounded SAM、LLaVA、CLIP和GPT）自动生成伪标签、过滤同义词并重建描述性字幕，通过三种训练目标增强视觉-文本对齐，在COCO数据集上的开放词汇实例分割和开放集全景分割任务中显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有开放词汇实例分割（OVIS）和开放集全景分割（OSPS）方法存在伪掩码噪声大、视觉-文本接地能力有限、难以处理同义词和词汇表外（OOV）词等问题，且依赖大量人工标注，亟需更有效的自动标注和跨模态对齐方案。

Method: 提出目标词汇辅助的伪标签生成多模态框架：1) 使用Grounded SAM生成伪分割掩码、LLaVA生成描述性字幕、CLIP进行同义词过滤，构建多模态监督信号；2) 通过三种互补训练目标增强视觉-文本对齐：扩展接地损失（融合视觉接地同义词）、语义一致性损失、生成式字幕重建损失。

Result: 在COCO数据集上的大量实验表明，该方法的OVIS和OSPS任务性能均持续优于现有最优方法，取得了显著的性能提升。

Conclusion: 通过预训练视觉-语言模型自动生成高质量多模态伪标注并配合多目标训练策略，能够有效解决开放词汇分割中的噪声伪掩码、弱跨模态对齐和同义词/OOV难题，为无需人工标注的开放集分割提供了可行路径。

Abstract: This work addresses the challenge of open-vocabulary instance segmentation (OVIS) and open-set panoptic segmentation (OSPS), which aim to recognize both predefined and unseen object categories without exhaustive human annotations. Existing methods often suffer from noisy pseudo-masks, limited visual-textual grounding, and difficulty handling synonyms or out-of-vocabulary (OOV) words.
