To support autonomous mechanistic discovery, we construct an interpretability-focused knowledge graph of approximately 13,000 papers and integrate it with a multidisciplinary database of 43 million papers spanning 26 fields. We further curate a library of 32 foundational methods for mechanism analysis, causal intervention, and validation. Compared with Claude Code and existing AI-scientist systems, Mechanist generates more valuable mechanism hypotheses and executes experiments more reliably. Mechanist also demonstrates a progression from discovering model behaviors to explaining and controlling AI models. Specifically, Mechanist first uncovers a counterintuitive safety risk in scientific laboratories, showing that unsafe traits can transfer across modalities through apparently safe training data. Mechanist then develops a mechanism theory of belief, revealing how models represent world knowledge, form beliefs, infer the beliefs of others, and how these mechanisms emerge during pretraining. Finally, Mechanist translates these mechanistic insights into practical interventions that improve model performance across diverse scenarios and steer scientific foundation models toward generating DNA sequences with specified properties.

</details>


### [170] [Graph-Structured Rubrics: Compiling Rubrics into Typed Evaluation Graphs for LLM Judges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12097)
*Xi Chen,Jie Mu,Mo Xuan,Qun Shao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出图结构评分标准（GSR），将评分标准编译为与响应无关的类型化评估图，通过命名端口组合标准节点与运算节点，并利用Readout输出映射生成分数或偏好，在多个基准上优于Prometheus风格评分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于评分标准的评估器通常将评分标准视为提示上下文或扁平标准，仅指定评判什么，却隐式地处理标准组合方式，即使自然语言规则中明确规定了组合逻辑，这种隐式处理会导致评估性能受限。

Method: GSR将评分标准编译为响应无关的类型化评估图：标准节点负责评判，变换/归约/门控算子通过命名端口对评判结果进行组合，任务特定的输出映射（Readout）将唯一汇聚节点转换为分数或偏好。编译过程会拒绝格式错误或类型不兼容的图。逐点评估先独立评判各标准维度再进行图聚合；成对评估复用同一图，每个候选在每个标准下进行一次评判。

Result: 在GPT-OSS-120B下，GSR在四个逐点数据集上相比Prometheus风格评分将精确分数一致性提高了0.62至6.75个百分点；在两个偏好基准上，在原生平局和弃权策略下达到了数值最高的端到端成对准确率。

Conclusion: 显式地将评分标准的组合逻辑建模为类型化图结构，可以显著提升大语言模型作为评估器的性能，无论是逐点评分还是成对偏好判断场景。

Abstract: Rubric-based evaluators commonly treat rubrics as prompt context or flat criteria: they specify what to judge but leave criterion composition implicit, even when natural-language rules state it. We introduce Graph-Structured Rubrics (GSR), which compiles a rubric into a response-independent typed evaluation graph before observing responses. Criterion nodes elicit judgments; transformation, reduction, and gating operators compose them through named ports; and a task-specific output mapping, termed Readout, converts the unique sink into a score or preference. Compilation rejects malformed or type-incompatible graphs. Pointwise evaluation judges rubric dimensions separately before graph aggregation; pairwise evaluation reuses the graph with one judgment for each candidate under every criterion. Under GPT-OSS-120B, GSR improves exact score agreement by 0.62--6.75 percentage points over Prometheus-style scoring on four pointwise datasets and achieves the numerically highest end-to-end pairwise accuracy on two preference benchmarks under native tie and abstention policies.

</details>


### [171] [GUIDE: Governed Unified Intelligence for Document-to-Artifact Generation in Enterprise Settings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12133)
*Shivali Dalmia,Sumukha Thoppanahalli,Mohammadreza Sediqin,Abhishek Mukherji*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: GUIDE是一个基于治理的多智能体框架，用于处理企业指南文档的解析、提取、验证和产物生成，在120份真实文档上实现96%的成功率，并将处理时间从2-3天缩短至40-125分钟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业指南文档异构且多模态（含文本、表格、图片），现有LLM/VLM系统存在幻觉、表格结构退化以及缺乏超越提取的治理工作流等问题，导致企业需手动处理每份文档2-3天。

Method: 构建GUIDE多智能体框架，包含共享版本化规则存储、模式验证的智能体间契约、端到端溯源追踪；六个专职智能体分别负责解析、VLM驱动提取、一致性检查、评估、人机协同升级和个性化产物合成。

Result: 在120份真实企业指南文档上评估，GUIDE实现96%文档成功率，提取3,896条规则（71.4%自动批准），生成812份可部署产物，每份文档处理时间缩短至40-125分钟。

Conclusion: GUIDE通过治理化多智能体协作与版本化规则管理，有效解决了企业指南文档处理的幻觉与流程治理问题，显著提升效率。

Abstract: Enterprise guideline documents are heterogeneous and multimodal, combining narrative text, complex tables, and embedded images. Existing LLM and VLM systems face hallucinated content, table structure degradation, and lack governed workflows extending beyond extraction to validation and artifact generation. This leaves enterprises to perform this manually, consuming 2-3 days per document. To address this, we introduce GUIDE, a governed multi-agent framework built on a shared versioned rule store with schema-validated inter-agent contracts and end-to-end provenance tracking. Six specialized agents handle parsing, VLM-driven extraction, consistency checking, evaluation, human-in-the-loop (HITL) escalation, and persona-tailored artifact synthesis. Evaluated on 120 real-world enterprise guideline documents, GUIDE achieves 96% document success, extracts 3,896 rules with 71.4% auto-approved, produces 812 deployment-ready artifacts, and reduces turnaround to 40-125 minutes per document.

</details>


### [172] [Who Thinks Best Depends on How Long You Let Them: Budget-Dependent Rankings in LLM Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12150)
*Rodrigo Guedes de Souza,Alison R. Panisson*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究挑战了大规模语言模型在不同推理条件下排名稳定的假设，通过在七个token预算级别（64-4,096）下对四个模型在三个推理基准上进行56,476次推理评估，发现模型排名会随预算变化而反转，提出了预算条件化的评估协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的大语言模型评估方法假设模型排名在不同推理条件下保持稳定，但这一假设缺乏实证检验。研究者希望探明token生成预算（模型可生成的最大token数）是否会影响模型性能排名，从而推动更严谨的评估协议。

Method: 在七个token预算级别（64到4,096）下，对四个大语言模型在三个推理基准上进行了共56,476次推理评估。通过控制截断效应、进行McNemar检验、Oracle分析以及构建预算感知路由器等方法，系统性地分析预算对模型性能和排名的影响。

Result: 四个主要发现：(i) 3-19%的项目呈非单调行为（预算增加反而精度下降），该现象具有模型特异性（跨模型重叠率仅6-14%）；(ii) 所有基准上模型排名随预算反转（p<0.01）；(iii) Oracle分析显示模型互补性最高达+27.8个百分点，在预算受限时最明显；(iv) 预算感知路由器跨域捕获Oracle差距的14.1%，预算特征在域内有效（+1.6至+5.7pp）但具域特异性且损害迁移（-1.2pp）。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，模型排名对token预算高度敏感，现有评估协议需要转变为预算条件化的评估方式，以更准确地反映模型在不同实际使用场景中的真实表现。

Abstract: Standard evaluation of large language models assumes stable model rankings across inference conditions. We challenge this assumption by varying the token generation budget, i.e., the maximum tokens a model may produce, across seven levels (64--4,096), evaluating four models on three reasoning benchmarks (56,476 inferences). We report four findings: (i) 3--19% of items exhibit non-monotone behavior (accuracy decreasing with more budget), even after controlling for truncation, and this phenomenon is model-specific (cross-model overlap: 6--14%). (ii) Model rankings reverse across budgets on all benchmarks ($p {<} 0.01$, McNemar). (iii) Oracle analysis reveals model complementarity up to $+27.8$pp, most pronounced at constrained budgets. (iv) A budget-aware router captures 14.1% of the oracle gap cross-domain; budget features help within-domain ($+1.6$ to $+5.7$pp) but are domain-specific and hurt transfer ($-1.2$pp). These results argue for budget-conditioned evaluation protocols.

</details>


### [173] [How to Spend Your Oracle Budget: Practical Guidance for Protein Structure Prediction Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12192)
*Aleksandra Kalisz,Jack Simons,Krisztina Sinkovics,Noam Ghenassia,Shikha Surana,Henry Moss,Paul Duckworth*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文首次系统比较了多种蛋白质结构预测的oracle预算感知引导方法，并扩展了O3方法到蛋白质结构预测领域，通过两个蛋白质靶标的实验发现没有单一方法在所有预算下都占优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 蛋白质结构预测的基础模型在某些目标上仍不可靠，需要外部oracle来标记和纠正错误，但生物oracle成本高昂，预算有限。现有引导方法（FK-steering、DPO、Best K-of-N）在预算消耗策略上各不相同，缺乏系统性比较来指导方法选择。

Method: 在蛋白质结构预测任务上对FK-steering、DPO、Best K-of-N三种已有方法进行基准测试，同时将Optimisation Over Outputs (O3)方法扩展应用到蛋白质结构预测模型，在钙调蛋白（1CLL）和大肠杆菌天冬氨酸转氨甲酰酶（9EEH）两个蛋白质靶标上进行评估。

Result: 在两种oracle和不同预算下，没有单一方法始终占优。O3在低oracle预算下最有效，而FK-steering和DPO在预算增加时表现提升。该工作在oracle预算感知引导方面提供了首个实用参考。

Conclusion: 研究将发现转化为面向实际oracle预算约束从业者的可操作建议，为在不同预算条件下选择合适的引导方法提供了指导。

Abstract: Foundation models for protein structure prediction remain unreliable on certain targets. External oracles can flag and correct these failures, but biological oracles are expensive, making oracle budget a critical constraint. Existing guidance methods, such as FK-steering, DPO, and Best K-of-N sampling, differ in how they spend this budget, yet no systematic comparison exists to guide method selection. To bridge this gap, we benchmark these methods alongside the recently proposed Optimisation Over Outputs (O3), which applies off-the-shelf optimisers within a generative model's latent subspace. We extend the usage of O3 to protein structure prediction models. Overall, our work provides the first practical reference for oracle budget-aware guidance. Our evaluation on two protein targets, calmodulin (1CLL) and E. coli aspartate transcarbamoylase (9EEH), reveals that no single method consistently dominates across all budgets and oracles. Specifically, O3 proves most effective at low oracle budgets, while FK-steering and DPO demonstrate improved performance as the budget increases. We distil these findings into actionable recommendations for practitioners operating under real-world oracle-budget constraints.

</details>


### [174] [An Agentic Workflow for Legacy HPC Modernization: Converting the Two-Electron-Integral Core of GAMESS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12249)
*Yuzhong Shen,Masha Sosonkina,Peng Xu,Mark S. Gordon*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种代理驱动的自动化工作流，用于大规模现代化遗留Fortran代码库，并在GAMESS量子化学软件包的两电子积分例程上进行了案例验证，成功将约5.6万行Fortran 77代码转换为Fortran 2008，522次测试运行中化学相关差异为零。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 遗留Fortran代码现代化面临"量"的挑战——单个体转换操作并不复杂，但代码规模庞大，在计算科学领域往往因工作量过大而被搁置。作者旨在探索自动化代理工作流能在多大程度上承担这一工作量。

Method: 设计了三个提示词特化代理角色，在版本控制的规范下协作（该规范由代理自身编写和修订），人类仅保留少量门控。利用领域内已有的精确验证预言机（GAMESS标准测试套件的逐位能量复制）作为安全边界。代理以Claude Code三种角色在隔离工作树中运行，跨越四代Claude模型。

Result: GAMESS的12个源文件、56,448行代码、225个子程序的Fortran 77到Fortran 2008转换全部完成，通过51项验证（49项标准GAMESS测试+2项额外计算），612次测试运行中化学相关差异为零，所有文件也通过Jenkins持续集成测试。

Conclusion: 安全委托的边界恰好位于验证预言机可见范围之处：当领域提供了精确的验证手段时，代理工作流可以在生产规模上可靠地执行遗留代码现代化，实现逐位精度保真的自动化转换。

Abstract: Modernizing legacy Fortran is a problem of volume: the transformations are individually routine, but the codebases can be enormous, and across much of computational science the work simply goes undone. We propose an agentic workflow that takes this work on at production scale, and we set out to measure how far such delegation can reach. In this work, three prompt-specialized agent roles operate under a version-controlled specification that the agents themselves authored and revised, while humans hold a small number of gates. The arrangement is kept safe by an exact verification oracle inherited from the domain, and the boundary of safe delegation lies exactly where that oracle stops seeing.
  We apply the proposed workflow in a case study, converting the two-electron-integral routines of GAMESS (General Atomic and Molecular Electronic Structure System), a mature quantum-chemistry package with a 48-year development history, from fixed-form Fortran 77 to free-form Fortran 2008. The scope of this work was twelve source files, 56,448 lines, and 225 subroutines for computing electron repulsion integrals. The agents ran as three Claude Code roles in isolated worktrees, and the work spanned four Claude model generations. Because the GAMESS group ships a standard test suite whose printed energies its user community treats as canonical, we could adopt bit-for-bit reproduction of those energies as the merge criterion, where a deviation in the twelfth decimal place counts as a failure rather than drift. All twelve source files pass a 51-test validation battery comprising the 49 standard GAMESS tests and two additional calculations, and across 612 test runs the number of chemistry-relevant differences is zero, and every file also passes the Jenkins tests that are used for continuous integration.

</details>


### [175] [VAKRA: Evaluating Multi-Hop Reasoning Across APIs and Retrieval Under Tool-Use Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12282)
*Ankita Rajaram Naik,Anupama Murthi,Benjamin Elder,Siyu Huo,Raavi Gupta,Abhinav Jain,Praveen Venkateswaran,Abdulhamid Adebayo,Danish Contractor*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: VAKRA是一个评估企业环境中API调用与知识检索相结合能力的基准测试，包含62000多个域中超过8000个可执行API，任务分为三个递增难度级别。实验发现即使最佳模型在简单任务上仅达70.4%，在复合API任务上降至50-51%，并且失败主要集中于语言中介推理环节（如实体消歧和跨源grounding），而非工具调用机制本身。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业环境中的智能体需要同时具备结构化API推理和文档检索能力，但现有基准测试将这两种能力割裂评估，无法反映真实场景中的综合需求。

Method: 构建包含62个域、超过8000个可执行API的VAKRA基准，设计三个递增难度的任务设置：多样化API交互风格、结构化API上的多跳推理、带自然语言工具使用策略约束的多源推理。通过重新执行预测的工具调用（而非模拟）对实时API进行正确性验证，支持多种有效路径，并使用固定ReAct框架隔离模型能力与智能体架构的影响。

Result: 最佳模型在单跳端点式任务上仅达到70.4%准确率，在复合API任务上降至50-51%；随着推理深度增加性能下降超过50%；策略约束问题暴露严重缺陷（不可回答查询上准确率低至2.4%）。轨迹分析显示失败集中在语言中介推理环节，即实体消歧和跨源grounding，而非工具调用机制本身。

Conclusion: 当前前端及开源模型在企业级API+知识检索综合场景中表现不足，尤其在策略约束和深层推理任务上存在显著短板，语言中介推理能力是制约智能体性能的关键瓶颈。

Abstract: Agents deployed in enterprise settings must reason across structured APIs and document collections, yet existing benchmarks evaluate these capabilities in isolation. We introduce VAKRA (e\textbf{V}aluating \textbf{A}PI and \textbf{K}nowledge \textbf{R}etrieval \textbf{A}gents), a benchmark of over $8{,}000$ executable APIs across $62$ domains with tasks spanning three settings of increasing difficulty: diverse API interaction styles, multi-hop reasoning over structured APIs, and multi-source reasoning with natural-language tool-use policy constraints. Correctness is verified by re-executing predicted tool calls against live APIs, accommodating multiple valid paths. Using a fixed ReAct harness to isolate model capabilities from agent architecture, we evaluate frontier and open-weight models and find that even the best model achieves only 70.4\% on single-hop endpoint-style tasks and drops to 50--51\% on compositional APIs; performance degrades by over 50\% as reasoning depth increases, and policy-constrained questions expose severe failures (as low as 2.4\% on unanswerable queries). Trace analysis shows failures concentrate at language-mediated reasoning - entity disambiguation, cross-source grounding, rather than tool invocation mechanics. Code is available https://github.com/IBM/VAKRA. Dataset is available https://huggingface.co/datasets/ibm-research/VAKRA

</details>


### [176] [Constructing Dynamic Master Logic Models as Knowledge Graphs for Complex System Diagnostics Using Retrieval-Augmented Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12304)
*Saman Marandi,Yu-Shu Hu,Mohammad Modarres*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个自动化框架，利用检索增强生成和大型语言模型，从系统描述中自动构建动态主逻辑（DML）模型并将其表示为知识图谱（KG-DML），应用于复杂系统（如沸水反应堆低压冷却剂注入系统），验证其在诊断推理和可靠性分析方面的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DML框架能通过连接功能目标与底层结构元素来表示系统行为，但传统构建依赖专家解读技术文档，难以扩展到复杂系统，需要自动化构建方法。

Method: 提出自动化KG-DML构建框架，基于检索增强生成和大语言模型，沿DML层级进行定向检索，保留功能依赖和逻辑关系；采用多层级验证方法评估精度、召回率、逻辑门一致性和结构完整性。

Result: 在退役沸水反应堆低压冷却剂注入系统上应用，多次运行结果一致，验证了自动化KG-DML构建的可靠性。

Conclusion: 自动化KG-DML构建可将技术文档转化为可执行的职能模型，支持诊断推理和可靠性分析，为大规模复杂系统的建模提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Dynamic Master Logic (DML) provides a hierarchical framework for representing system behavior by linking functional objectives to underlying structural elements. However, DML construction typically relies on expert interpretation of technical documentation, limiting scalability for complex systems. This study presents a framework for automated construction of DML models from system descriptions and their representation as Knowledge Graphs (KG-DML), using Retrieval-Augmented Generation and Large Language Models as enabling tools. Building on prior work with small-scale systems, the framework extends automated KG-DML construction and evaluation to substantially larger and more complex systems. Model construction proceeds across the DML hierarchy using targeted retrieval while preserving functional dependencies and explicit logical relationships. The resulting KG-DML supports diagnostic reasoning, safety assessment, upward failure propagation, and downward dependency tracing. A multi-level validation methodology evaluates layer-specific precision and recall, logical gate consistency, and overall structural integrity. Application to the Low-Pressure Coolant Injection system of a decommissioned Boiling Water Reactor demonstrates consistent reconstruction across repeated runs. The results show that automated KG-DML construction can transform technical documentation into executable functional models for diagnostic and reliability analysis.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [177] [Adaptation of Generalist Robot Policies with Minimal Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11363)
*Shreyas Kowshik,Sreyas Venkataraman,Leo Wang,Niharika Pant,Max Simchowitz,Aviral Kumar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出MiDAS方法，通过"单/少量演示的行为克隆+残差策略上的基于价值的在线强化学习"这一简单配方，实现预训练机器人策略从单条演示开始进行自主在线学习与改进，并在LIBERO、RoboCasa仿真环境及双机械臂YAM真实平台上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人学习的目标是摆脱对任务特定人类数据的依赖，实现自主交互式改进。但完全自主学习仍面临稀疏奖励和弱探索的挑战，机器人几乎不可能从零开始发现成功行为。作者研究"最小数据适应"这一设定：预训练策略仅需一条演示即可启动自主在线学习，这是完全自主改进最接近的可行代理，目标是探究最小人类引导能否启动自主学习以及需要什么算法要素。

Method: MiDAS是一个从离线到在线的强化学习配方，包含两个阶段：1) 先用行为克隆在单条/少量演示上对预训练的VLA（视觉-语言-动作）模型进行锚定，使其适应目标任务；2) 然后通过基于价值的在线强化学习，在残差策略参数化上对策略进行改进。残差策略保留了预训练模型的先验知识，在线RL则在细粒度上修正和提升行为。

Result: 在LIBERO和RoboCasa基准上，MiDAS仅凭单条演示就能恢复强任务性能，显著优于基线方法，并能泛化到超出演示条件的新场景。在双机械臂YAM真实平台上，从单条演示获得的脆弱低成功率策略出发，MiDAS经过约6小时的在线交互提升了鲁棒性并学到了新的成功行为。

Conclusion: MiDAS首次证明了从单条任务演示实现可靠的机器人策略适应是可行的。该工作表明，"少量人类引导+自主在线交互"可以作为完全自主改进的实用替代路径，为机器人学习从任务特定数据收集走向自主改进提供了重要一步。

Abstract: A central goal in robot learning is to move beyond task-specific human data collection toward robots that improve through autonomous interaction. Yet fully autonomous learning remains difficult with current policies: sparse rewards and weak zero-shot exploration make it unlikely that a robot will discover successful behavior from scratch. We study minimal-data adaptation, a regime in which a pre-trained robot policy must learn a new task from as little as one demonstration followed by autonomous online interaction. This setting serves as the closest tractable proxy for fully autonomous improvement, allowing us to study whether minimal human guidance can bootstrap autonomous learning and what algorithmic ingredients make it feasible. We build MiDAS, a simple offline-to-online RL recipe that first anchors a pre-trained VLA to the target task with behavior cloning on single/few demonstrations, then improves it through value-based online RL on a residual policy parameterization. Across LIBERO and RoboCasa, MiDAS recovers strong task performance from as little as one demonstration, substantially outperforming baselines and generalizing beyond demonstrated conditions. We further evaluate MiDAS on a bimanual YAM platform. Starting from a fragile low-success policy obtained from a single demonstration, MiDAS improves its robustness and learns new successful behaviors over ~6 hours of online interaction. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first demonstration of reliable robot policy adaptation from a single task demonstration.

</details>


### [178] [Top-down Traffic Scenario Generation via Joint Initial-Goal Diffusion and Trajectory Infilling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11407)
*Da Saem Lee,Yash Vardhan Pant,Sebastian Fischmeister*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出TrafficDiffuser，一个端到端的交通场景生成框架，通过联合建模初始状态和目标状态对来生成高层交通场景，使初始状态更具可解释性，并将轨迹生成简化为填充问题。在Argoverse 2数据集上评估，该方法在速度分布距离上比次优方法降低55.3%，离路率降低2.8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的交通模拟器需要代理的初始状态来生成轨迹，这限制了数据集的规模性和多样性。虽然数据驱动的代理初始化已有研究，但生成的初始状态在位置选择上缺乏可解释性；同时已知初始状态后的轨迹生成也面临目的地变异性和时间到达的挑战。

Method: 提出TrafficDiffuser框架，联合建模高层交通场景（由初始状态和目标状态对定义）。通过联合建模使初始状态更具可解释性，并将轨迹生成简化为填充问题。支持基于不同轨迹模式的约束以及与现有轨迹生成模型的集成。

Result: 在Argoverse 2运动预测数据集上进行了广泛实验。在生成目标状态的基础上，TrafficDiffuser在代理初始化方面优于次优方法，速度分布距离降低了55.3%，离路率降低了2.8%。

Conclusion: TrafficDiffuser通过联合建模初始和目标状态，成功提高了交通场景生成的可解释性和真实性，为自动驾驶测试提供了更有效的模拟数据生成方案。

Abstract: Robust traffic simulators are crucial for developing and testing autonomous vehicles to reduce the costly, labor-intensive real-world data collection process and the need for physical presence on the road. However, existing simulators require agents' initial states to generate trajectories, which limits scalability and diversity due to restrictions on the given initial states. While data-driven agent initialization has been widely studied, the generated initial states are not interpretable in terms of why the agents are initialized at those specific locations. Given known initial states, trajectory generation is also a challenging problem, as the model must learn the variability of the destination and how agents should reach it over time. In this paper, we propose TrafficDiffuser, a top-down traffic scenario generation framework that generates high-level traffic scenarios, defined by initial and goal state pairs, by jointly modeling them. The high-level scenario generation makes initial states better interpretable and reduces trajectory generation into as simple as an infilling problem. We demonstrate how the generated high-level traffic scenarios can be used, including constraining based on different trajectory modes and integrating them with existing trajectory generation models. We conduct extensive experiments on the Argoverse 2 motion prediction dataset to evaluate how well the generated outputs capture real-world distributions. In addition to generating goal states, TrafficDiffuser outperforms the next-best approach for agent initialization, reducing speed distribution distance by 55.3% and the off-road rate by 2.8%.

</details>


### [179] [From Self-Normal-Positioning to Omni-Directional Tracking: Real-Time Surface Modeling Enabled Probe Tilt Control for Robotic Ultrasound Imaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11409)
*Xihan Ma,Haichong Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种全向探头方向控制框架，集成RGB-D感知、局部表面建模和任务空间方向控制，使机器人超声探头能够以任意角度（包括非垂直角度）进行成像，解决了现有机器人超声系统仅支持垂直探头方向的局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人超声系统大多限制探头垂直于局部表面，这对需要特定非垂直角度才能获得诊断视图的检查（如超声心动图）是不够的，临床需要能够实时感知局部表面并保持任意期望探头方向的控制系统。

Method: 提出全向探头方向控制框架，包含三个核心模块：RGB-D感知用于多视角点云融合，局部表面建模提供局部表面的二次估计，任务空间方向控制将期望成像方向相对于表面法线编码，实现任意角度的探头跟踪。

Result: 平均角度跟踪误差为1.06±0.66度；系统可恢复相对于表面法线最大44.39±2.59度的非垂直倾斜角；在体模和体内实验中成功获取期望的心脏腔室视图。

Conclusion: 该框架能有效实现全方向的探头方向控制，突破了传统垂直约束，在需要非垂直入射角度的超声检查（如超声心动图）中具有临床应用潜力。

Abstract: Ultrasound (US) provides real-time, radiation-free imaging, but the image quality depends strongly on how the probe is oriented against the patient body. Robotic US can reduce operator workload and improve acquisition consistency; however, most existing systems focus on normal positioning, where the probe is maintained perpendicular to the local surface. This constraint is inadequate for examinations like echocardiography, where obtaining a diagnostic view requires a non-normal probe angle. Consequently, a clinically useful robotic system must sense the local surface in real-time and preserve the desired probe orientation. Here, we propose an omni-directional probe-orientation control framework that integrates RGB-D perception, local-surface modeling, and task-space orientation control. The surface model fuses multi-view point clouds and provides a quadratic estimate of the local surface. A desired imaging direction is then encoded relative to the normal, enabling the probe to track arbitrary angles. The framework was evaluated through flat-surface tracking, phantom target-angle recovery, and in-vivo tracking of an expert selected view. Results show that the mean angular tracking error was 1.06 +- 0.66 deg. The system recovered a non-normal tilt angle of up to 44.39 +- 2.59 deg relative to the surface normal, and acquired the desired heart chamber view in the phantom and in-vivo experiments.

</details>


### [180] [Locomotion Variability and User Experience in Smart Wheelchair Human-Robot Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11417)
*Sean Kille,Adina M. Panchea,Balint Varga,Sören Hohmann*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究了共享控制中不同辅助策略对人类运动变异性、任务表现及主观交互体验的影响，提出了一种保留用户自然运动结构的自主支持型共享控制策略。用户研究（电动车轮椅推动任务）表明，与传统降低变异性的辅助方式相比，保留运动变异性的策略在任务表现相当的情况下显著提升了用户感知代理感和感知有用性，支持了变异性感知辅助系统在物理人机协作中的价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人机交互中的模型辅助策略通常假设人类行为是确定性的，从而抑制了人类运动固有的变异性，这可能影响交互体验并降低代理感。尽管运动变异性在功能上具有重要意义，但如何在辅助交互中有意保留它及其对用户体验的影响尚未得到充分探索。

Method: 提出一种自主支持型共享控制策略以保留用户的自然运动结构，并通过用户研究进行实证评估。研究设置了三种条件：无辅助、传统降低变异性的辅助、保留变异性的辅助，参与者推动智能动力轮椅完成相关任务，同时测量运动变异性、任务表现和主观交互体验。

Result: 两种辅助模式在任务相关表现上相当，但保留自然运动变异性的辅助方式带来了更积极的交互体验，参与者报告了显著更高的感知代理感（相比传统辅助）以及最高的感知有用性。

Conclusion: 变异性感知的辅助策略能够在保持性能的同时支持用户自主性，强调辅助机器人系统设计应尊重人类运动的具身结构，而非将变异性视为需要抑制或消除的噪声。

Abstract: Human movement is inherently variable, with variability structured according to task relevance: movements are typically more consistent at task-critical points and more flexible elsewhere. In human-robot interaction (HRI), however, model-based assistance strategies commonly assume deterministic human behavior and suppress such variability, potentially altering how interactions are experienced and lowering sense of agency. While movement variability is increasingly recognized as functionally meaningful, its deliberate preservation in assisted interaction, and its consequences for user experience, remain underexplored. In this paper, we empirically investigate how different assistance strategies shape human movement variability, task performance, and subjective interaction experience in a shared control setting. We introduce an autonomy-supportive shared control strategy that preserves users' natural movement structure. This approach is evaluated in a user study in which participants push an intelligent powered wheelchair under three conditions: no assistance, conventional variability-reducing assistance, and variability-preserving assistance. While task-relevant performance remained comparable across assisted modes, preserving natural movement variability led to more favorable interaction experiences. In particular, participants reported significantly higher perceived agency compared to conventional assistance and highest perceived usefulness. These findings suggest that variability-aware assistance can support both performance and user autonomy in physical human-robot collaboration. More broadly, the results highlight the importance of designing assistive robotic systems that respect the embodied structure of human movement rather than treating variability as noise to be neglected or eliminated.

</details>


### [181] [Herding End-to-End Autonomous Driving via Neuro-Symbolic Safety Guards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11451)
*Simón Patiño Idarraga,Erick Silva,Rehana Yasmin,Ali Shoker*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一个神经符号安全卫士模块，附着在已训练好的端到端驾驶智能体的指令接口上，在命令到达车辆前根据显式安全规则检查并替换为最近的安全替代方案，无需重新训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代端到端驾驶智能体平均表现优秀，但仍会违反人类驾驶员绝不会犯的基本交通规则。其根本原因是结构性的：模型学习的是统计模式而非保证安全驾驶的物理条件，导致决策过程不透明、安全约束无法强制实施。

Method: 设计了一个轻量级的神经符号安全卫士模块，附着于已训练智能体的最终指令接口。在命令到达车辆前的瞬间，该模块将命令与显式安全规则进行比对，仅在必要时将其替换为最近的安全替代方案。每次干预都可直接执行，且可追溯到触发它的规则；卫士本身无需重新训练，也不增加任何学习组件。

Result: 在Fail2Drive和Bench2Drive长尾基准测试中，以SOTA方法TransFuser v6（TFv6）为案例，该安全卫士将成功率（Success Rate）提升了15%，将安全关键碰撞减少了最多53%，同时保持了原有的驾驶评分（Driving Score）。

Conclusion: 神经符号安全卫士通过显式规则检查在不牺牲驾驶评分的情况下显著提升了端到端驾驶智能体的安全性和可解释性，是一种轻量、无需重新训练且干预可追踪的有效安全防护方案。

Abstract: Modern end-to-end driving agents can achieve high average performance yet still violate basic traffic rules that a human driver would never miss. The reason is structural: they learn statistical patterns rather than the physical conditions that guarantee safe driving, leaving their decision-making process opaque and safety constraints unenforced. We introduce a neuro-symbolic safety guard, a lightweight module that attaches to the final command interface of an already-trained agent. Immediately before a command reaches the vehicle, it checks the command against explicit safety rules and, only when necessary, replaces it with the nearest safe alternative. Each intervention is directly executable and traceable to the rule that triggered it, while the guard itself requires no retraining and adds no learned component. Evaluated on the long-tail benchmarks Fail2Drive and Bench2Drive using the state-of-the-art TransFuser v6 (TFv6) as a case study, the guard improves Success Rate by 15% and reduces safety-critical collisions by up to 53%, while preserving the original Driving Score.

</details>


### [182] [Koopman Representation of Nonlinear Virtual Environments in Kinesthetic Haptic Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11461)
*Yanting Zhou,Jozsef Kövecses,James Richard Forbes*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出利用Koopman算子表示非线性虚拟环境（VE）以用于触觉反馈渲染，并通过仿真和实验验证了其对Duffing振荡器VE非线性动力学的有效表示，同时利用该表示进行闭环稳定性分析，相比传统基于无源性的方法更不保守，且对触觉设备建模不确定性更具鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在许多需要高精度力反馈的应用中，对非线性虚拟环境进行触觉反馈渲染非常重要，但传统的表示和稳定性分析方法存在保守性过高等问题。

Method: 采用Koopman算子将非线性VE线性化表示，利用所有提升状态的线性组合来表征非线性动力学，并基于该表示对整体触觉系统进行闭环稳定性分析。

Result: 仿真和实验结果表明该方法能有效表示Duffing振荡器VE的非线性动力学，多用户研究进一步证实了该结论；闭环稳定性分析比传统无源性方法更不保守，且对设备建模不确定性更鲁棒。

Conclusion: Koopman算子提供了一种表示非线性虚拟环境的新途径，既能实现有效的触觉力反馈，又便于进行更宽松的闭环稳定性分析，并增强了对建模不确定性的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Rendering haptic feedback with nonlinear virtual environments (VEs) is important in many applications that require highly accurate force feedback. This paper considers the use of the Koopman operator to represent a nonlinear VE interacting with a haptic system. Simulation and experimental results demonstrated that the proposed method provides an effective representation of the nonlinear dynamics of a Duffing-oscillator VE. A multi-user study further confirmed this conclusion. In addition, a closed-loop (CL) stability analysis is performed leveraging the Koopman representation of the nonlinear VE to access stability of the overall haptic system. This alternative way of representing nonlinear VEs enables a convenient CL stability analysis that is less conservative than traditional passivity-based methods. Since a linear combination of all lifted states is used to represent the nonlinearity, such representation is also more robust to uncertainties in the modeling of the haptic device than a traditional nonlinear model.

</details>


### [183] [Keep the Future, Drop the Rollout: RIFT for World Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11521)
*Chushan Zhang,Jinguang Tong,Xuesong Li,Yikai Wang,Hongdong Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出RIFT模型，通过学习未来标记（anticipation tokens）在一次主干网络前向传播中构建完整的未来K/V缓存，替代世界动作模型（WAMs）中迭代式视频推演（video rollout）来预测未来，在保持相近成功率的同时将动作块延迟降低68.2%~89.1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界动作模型（WAMs）通过预测未来来调节机器人动作，但迭代式视频推演会显著增加部署延迟。作者质疑动作生成是否真的需要完整的推演轨迹，还是仅依赖于其未来表示，从而寻求消除部署时迭代视频生成的方案。

Method: 首先通过配对闭环干预实验验证WAMs对未来缓存值及其位置的敏感性；然后提出RIFT方法，利用学习得到的未来标记在一次骨干网络前向传播中构建完整的未来K/V缓存，同时保留原有未来读取接口，实现无需推演的（rollout-free）未来条件化。

Result: 在LIBERO基准上，RIFT达到98.8%的成功率，接近基于推演的Joint、IDM和LingBot-VA（98.4%~98.6%），同时动作块延迟降低68.2%~89.1%。在RoboTwin 2.0上，RIFT在干净/随机场景中分别达到92.9%/92.6%的成功率，为评估方法中的最高水平。

Conclusion: 结果表明，缓存消费与缓存生产是可分离的：模型可以复用固定缓存但仍需迭代推演来构建它。RIFT证明了无需迭代视频生成即可实现无推演的未来条件化，显著提升部署效率且不损失性能。

Abstract: World action models (WAMs) condition robot actions on predicted futures, but iterative video rollout increases deployment latency. We ask whether action generation requires the evolving rollout trajectory or only its future representation. Across four WAMs on all 40 LIBERO tasks, paired closed-loop interventions show that masking or reassigning future-cache values changes execution and reduces success, indicating sensitivity to future values and their assigned positions. For Joint and Cosmos-2, however, replaying one fixed final-clean key/value (K/V) cache nearly preserves unmodified execution, with $1.7$ to $1.9$~cm end-effector average displacement error and $97.9\%$ to $98.2\%$ success. This separates cache consumption from production: these models can reuse a fixed cache but still require iterative rollout to construct it. We therefore propose RIFT (\emph{Rollout-free Imagination via Future Tokens}), which uses learned anticipation tokens to construct a complete future K/V cache in one backbone pass while retaining the original future-read interface. On LIBERO, RIFT achieves $98.8\%$ success, close to rollout-based Joint, IDM, and LingBot-VA at $98.4\%$ to $98.6\%$, while reducing action-chunk latency by $68.2\%$ to $89.1\%$. On RoboTwin~2.0, RIFT reaches $92.9/92.6\%$ on clean/randomized scenes, the highest observed among the evaluated methods. These results support rollout-free future conditioning without iterative video generation at deployment.

</details>


### [184] [RoadWeaver: Large-Scale Lane-Level HD Map Generation from Scratch for Autonomous Driving Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11580)
*Yueyuan Li,Zexi Chen,Weijie Xi,Mingyang Jiang,Songan Zhang,Hanyang Zhuang,Ming Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RoadWeaver提出了一种从粗到细的框架，用于从头生成多样化的、大规模的车道级高清地图，在1.39-3.50秒内生成完整HD地图，端点对齐误差较SOTA方法降低94.4%，可直接部署于自动驾驶仿真器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶仿真需要多样化和可扩展的车道级高清地图来支持复杂路网上的长时程评估，但现有方法要么依赖手工或重建的真实地图（可扩展性差），要么只能生成局部道路结构而非完整HD地图。

Method: RoadWeaver采用粗到细的三阶段框架：首先合成全局道路布局，然后将其扩展为连通的道路网络，最后构建具有拓扑一致车道连通性的车道级几何结构。

Result: 达到99.8%的可达性、10.7%的死端比率和0.24米的端点对齐误差；与SOTA生成方法相比，端点对齐误差降低94.4%，并在1.39-3.50秒内生成完整HD地图。

Conclusion: RoadWeaver生成的地图可直接部署在驾驶仿真器中，为自动驾驶系统的未来闭环评估提供可扩展的仿真环境。

Abstract: Autonomous driving simulation requires diverse and scalable lane-level HD maps to support long-horizon evaluation across complex road networks. Existing approaches either rely on handcrafted or reconstructed real-world maps, which limits scalability, or generate only local road structures rather than complete HD maps. We present RoadWeaver, a coarse-to-fine framework for from-scratch generation of diverse, large-scale HD maps. RoadWeaver first synthesizes a global road layout, expands it into a connected road network, and then constructs lane-level geometry with topologically consistent lane connectivity. Experimental results show that RoadWeaver achieves a 99.8\% reachability, a 10.7\% dead-end ratio, and an endpoint alignment error of 0.24 m. Compared with SOTA generation methods, it reduces endpoint alignment error by 94.4\% while generating complete HD maps in 1.39--3.50 s. The generated maps can be directly deployed in driving simulators, providing scalable simulation environments for future closed-loop evaluation of autonomous driving systems. The training code and an out-of-the-box implementation of RoadWeaver will be released upon acceptance.

</details>


### [185] [Video2Track: From Real-World Interaction Videos to Steerable Adversarial Closed-Track Testing for Automated Driving Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11592)
*Mengjie Tian,Xinrui Zhang,Tianyu Li,Peizhi Zhang,Guirong Zhou,Haojie Feng,Junpeng Huang,Qixiang Zhang,Lu Xiong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Video2Track框架，将真实世界驾驶视频中的交互场景转移到封闭赛道测试中，通过场景语义映射和动态交互测试两个模块，实现可操控的对抗性封闭赛道测试。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有封闭赛道测试多依赖标准化协议或预定义轨迹，导致交互过于脚本化，难以复现真实道路交通的复杂性和自然性。

Method: Video2Track包含两个紧密耦合的模块：(1) 场景语义映射模块，利用视觉语言模型从驾驶视频提取结构化语义，通过检索增强生成将其映射到封闭赛道拓扑库中，识别兼容地图段和交互锚点；(2) 动态交互测试模块，基于已映射的拓扑和锚点，通过条件扩散模型生成多样化的多智能体轨迹，并利用Stackelberg博弈和参数化对抗目标调节交互强度。

Result: 封闭赛道实验表明，该框架能够忠实复现具有代表性的真实世界交互场景，并生成可控风险水平和交互风格的可执行场景变体，为真实且可操控的自动驾驶系统验证提供了可扩展的方案。

Conclusion: Video2Track为自动驾驶系统验证提供了一种从真实视频到可控封闭赛道测试的可扩展方法，兼具真实性与可操纵性。

Abstract: Closed-track testing plays a fundamental role in the verification and validation of automated driving systems (ADS), particularly for safety-critical scenarios, by enabling reproducible evaluation under controlled conditions. However, most existing approaches still rely on standardized protocols or predefined trajectories, leading to overly scripted interactions and limited ability to reproduce the natural complexity of public-road traffic. To address this limitation, we propose Video2Track, a framework that transfers real-world interactive driving scenarios from videos into steerable adversarial closed-track testing. The framework consists of two tightly coupled modules. The first is a scenario semantic mapping module, which extracts structured semantics from driving videos using a vision-language model and grounds them onto a closed-track topology library via retrieval-augmented generation, thereby identifying compatible map segments and interaction anchors. The second is a dynamic interactive testing module, which conditions on the grounded topology and anchors to generate diverse multi-agent trajectories through a conditional diffusion model, while regulating interaction intensity via a Stackelberg game with a parameterized adversarial objective. Closed-track experiments demonstrate that the proposed framework can faithfully reproduce representative real-world interaction scenarios and generate executable scenario variants with controllable risk levels and interaction styles, providing a scalable approach for realistic and steerable ADS validation.

</details>


### [186] [IoT-Enabled Autonomous Maritime Navigation in Smart Ports: A Curriculum-Guided Shared Policy Learning Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11597)
*Yuqing Lin,Rangya Zhang,Kum Fai Yuen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种课程引导的强化学习框架，结合共享循环策略，用于物联网驱动的自主海事设备在部分可观测和密集交通条件下的船载自主导航，提升导航可靠性、避碰能力和部署可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智慧港口基础设施日益依赖物联网赋能的自主海事设备，在拥堵航道中确保可靠的船载导航智能成为安全可扩展运营的关键挑战，特别是在部分可观测性和密集交通条件下。

Method: 采用课程引导的强化学习框架，引入共享循环策略以增强时序推理能力；采用集中训练作为离线设计策略，所有导航动作均在船载边缘端执行，符合物联网边缘智能范式。

Result: 在多个真实港口环境中的大量仿真表明，相比标准基线方法，该方法在导航可靠性、避碰能力和训练稳定性方面均有提升，并能有效泛化到未见过的超高密度场景。

Conclusion: 课程引导的共享学习为智慧港口运营中物联网自主海事设备的可扩展部署提供了实用且有效的解决方案。

Abstract: As smart port infrastructures increasingly rely on autonomous maritime devices enabled by the Internet of Things (IoT), ensuring reliable onboard navigation intelligence has become a critical challenge for safe and scalable operations in congested waterways. This paper investigates onboard autonomous navigation for such IoT devices under partial observability and dense traffic conditions. A curriculum-guided reinforcement learning framework with a shared recurrent policy is developed to enhance temporal reasoning, deployment scalability, and robustness of edge-level decision-making. Centralized training is adopted as an offline design-time strategy, while all navigation actions are executed fully onboard, consistent with IoT edge intelligence paradigms. Extensive simulations in multiple realistic port environments demonstrate that the proposed approach improves navigation reliability, collision avoidance, and training stability compared with standard baseline methods, and generalizes effectively to previously unseen high-density scenarios. The results indicate that curriculum-guided shared learning provides a practical solution for scalable deployment of IoT-enabled autonomous maritime devices in smart port operations.

</details>


### [187] [StellaVLA: In-Context Structured Demonstration for Generalizable Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11671)
*Siyu Xu,Yunke Wang,Zijian Wang,Dihao Zhu,Chenghao Xia,Chengbin Du,Daochang Liu,Tao Huang,Chang Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: StellaVLA是一个视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型框架，通过检索单个演示的结构化信息（任务计划、子目标描述、3D运动语言化）作为上下文指导，在测试时实现分布外（OOD）场景的零样本适应，无需收集新数据或微调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型在训练数据分布之外的场景（场景、视角或物体变化）中性能会急剧下降，而传统适应方式需要收集更多数据和微调，成本高昂且效率低下。因此需要一种能够在测试时低成本适应的新方法。

Method: StellaVLA将原始轨迹通过自动化离线管线转化为结构化演示（包含任务计划、子目标描述和3D运动的口头化描述），无需人工标注。这些结构化演示作为上下文指导输入策略，让模型理解任务推理而非简单模仿像素轨迹。采用并行双训练设计，训练时联合行动与语言目标，推理时仅使用行动分支，保持实时高频控制。

Result: StellaVLA在VLA-Arena排行榜（2026年8月1日）上以0.63综合得分排名第一，显著优于π₀.₅的0.44和LingBot-VLA的0.22。在LIBERO上达到98.8%平均成功率，LIBERO-Plus上达到85.1%成功率。真实机器人实验证明其能够使用人类/机器人演示及XR演示作为上下文指导来适应OOD任务。

Conclusion: StellaVLA通过将"专家做了什么"的模仿升级为"为什么这样做"的推理，实现了跨实体（真实机器人、人类手部、XR演示）的可迁移性和测试时OOD适应能力，无需额外数据或微调，同时在多个基准测试上取得领先性能。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models can follow instructions and manipulate objects, but their performance often collapses out of distribution (OOD), when the scene, viewpoint, or object differs from training. Adapting to each new situation typically requires collecting more data and fine-tuning. We present StellaVLA, a framework that instead adapts at test time by conditioning on a single retrieved demonstration. The key idea is to move beyond imitating what an expert did and instead convey why: an automated offline pipeline converts each raw trajectory into a structured demonstration, e.g., a task plan, sub-goal descriptions, and verbalized 3D motion, at zero human-annotation cost. Provided as in-context guidance, this structured demonstration lets the policy reason about the task rather than mimic a pixel trajectory, which also makes it transferable across embodiments (real-robot, human-hand, or XR demonstrations). A parallel dual-training design internalizes this reasoning during training through a joint action-and-language objective, while inference uses the action expert alone, preserving real-time, high-frequency control with no added latency. On the VLA-Arena leaderboard(Aug 1, 2026), StellaVLA ranks first with an overall score of 0.63, versus 0.44 and 0.22 for the strong prior models ($π_{0.5}$ and LingBot-VLA), and it further leads on LIBERO with 98.8% average success rate and LIBERO-Plus with 85.1% success rate. Our real-robot benchmark demonstrates that StellaVLA can use both human/robot demos and human-to-robot (XR) demos as in-context structured demonstration to help VLA model adapt to OOD tasks.

</details>


### [188] [ContactIPM: A Structure-Exploiting Interior-Point Solver for Contact-Implicit Trajectory Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11731)
*Yucheng Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出ContactIPM，一种结合接触感知的原始-对偶内点法，通过弹性屏障松弛和Riccati递归求解接触隐式轨迹优化问题（MPCC），在多个基准测试中相比CRISP和IMPACT实现显著加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 接触隐式轨迹优化虽无需预设接触时序，但其MPCC退化性挑战传统原始-对偶求解器。现有接触专用方法未利用分阶段最优控制分解和原始-对偶一致性，而结构利用型最优控制求解器又不支持互补约束，两者能力无法兼顾。

Method: ContactIPM识别互补不等式对，通过屏障耦合弹性内部松弛嵌入它们，分阶段消除松弛变量和对偶变量，并使用Riccati递归求解约简Newton系统。采用固定多阶段MPCC恢复调度提供四种延拓和重启尝试，终止由未松弛的物理互补残差门控。

Result: 在四个CRISP基准案例中，ContactIPM快2.17-8.87倍，在Push Box和Push-T鲁棒性套件上成功率更高。对比IMPACT，Push T上快2.96倍，Cart Transport上快4.91倍，但Push Box上慢4.46倍。在50次闭环Push Box滚动中涉及模型失配、测量噪声、初始位姿误差和状态重置。

Conclusion: ContactIPM证明接触专用方法和结构利用型最优控制求解器的能力可以结合在单一原始-对偶方法中，在多数基准上实现显著速度和成功率提升，但个别场景下性能仍需改进。

Abstract: Contact-implicit trajectory optimization avoids prescribing contact sequences, but yields mathematical programs with complementarity constraints (MPCCs) whose degeneracy challenges conventional primal--dual solvers. Existing contact-specific methods improve robustness to this degeneracy but do not leverage a stagewise optimal-control factorization and primal--dual consistency, while structure-exploiting optimal-control solvers are not designed for complementarity constraints. We show that these capabilities can be combined in a single primal--dual method. ContactIPM identifies complementary inequality pairs, embeds them through a barrier-coupled elastic interior relaxation, eliminates slack and dual variables stagewise, and solves the reduced Newton system using a Riccati recursion. A fixed multi-phase MPCC recovery schedule provides four continuation and restart attempts from naive initializations, while termination is gated by the unrelaxed physical complementarity residual.
  We compare ContactIPM with two contact-specific MPCC solvers, CRISP and IMPACT, using matched benchmark conditions and common post-solve acceptance criteria. On four fixed CRISP benchmark cases, ContactIPM is $2.17$--$8.87\times$ faster over 20 paired timing repetitions per case and achieves higher success on the Push Box and Push-T robustness suites. Against IMPACT, ContactIPM is \(2.96\times\) faster on Push T and \(4.91\times\) faster on Cart Transport, but \(4.46\times\) slower on Push Box. In 50 closed-loop Push Box rollouts spanning model mismatch, measurement noise, initial-pose errors, and state resets,

</details>


### [189] [G0.5: One Autoregressive Stream for Robot Reasoning and Action](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11739)
*Yicheng Liu,Zibin Dong,Baijun Ye,Tianyuan Yuan,Tao Jiang,Anqi Yang,Shicheng Cao,Haonan Liu,Yue Sun,Zihan Guo,Xiao Liu,Dong Ke,Changxun Pan,Chenru Wu,Tailai Cheng,Xiaoshu Ren,Xinlei Zhang,Jianning Cui,Zijie Zhao,Haoyu Zhang,Kaiming Xu,Haodong Yang,Bowen Zhang,Jiahui Niu,Shaoting Zhu,Shiduo Zhang,Hang Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出G0.5，一种预训练的纯自回归视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型。与现有方法将VLM作为上下文编码器、另配单独的动作专家不同，G0.5使用单一Transformer解码器，在统一目标下同时生成推理token和动作token。通过跨实体动作分词器、原生思维链流和视觉记忆模块三大组件，使该架构在基础模型规模下可行。实验表明G0.5在7个独立基准上超越现有SOTA模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型的范式是将预训练VLM与单独训练的流匹配动作专家结合，这导致VLM仅充当上下文编码器而非真正的决策者，限制了模型将自身能力直接迁移到物理行为上。

Method: G0.5采用三个关键组件：1) 可学习的跨实体动作分词器，将异构机器人动作映射到共享词汇表；2) 原生思维链流，将任务分解、目标定位和动作提示与动作token交错排列；3) 视觉记忆模块，通过视觉编码器注入多秒历史信息。推理和动作共享同一套权重，使预训练VLM的能力自然迁移到物理行为。

Result: G0.5在7个独立基准上超越SOTA：真实机器人微调（R1lite/R1pro上76.7% vs π0.5的53.3%和GR00T-N1.7的24.4%）、2025 BEHAVIOR Challenge（31.4% vs 26.3%/26.1%）、DROID零样本迁移（82.5%）、LIBERO（98.9%）、RoboTwin 2.0（93.3%）、SimplerEnv-Bridge（87.3%）等。

Conclusion: G0.5证明了通过单一自回归目标统一推理与动作生成是可行的，且推理能力与动作能力的共享权重能带来显著的性能提升。模型的提示直接调控动作粒度、任务范围和非分布场景处理，无需额外训练即可泛化。

Abstract: The prevailing recipe for Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models couples a pretrained VLM with a separately trained flow-matching action expert. This makes the VLM a context encoder rather than a decision-maker. We introduce G0.5, a pretrained autoregressive VLA in which a single transformer decoder emits reasoning and action tokens under a single objective. Three components make this tractable at foundation-model scale: a learnable cross-embodiment action tokenizer that maps heterogeneous robot actions into a shared vocabulary; a native chain-of-thought stream interleaving task decomposition, object grounding, and action hints with action tokens; and a visual memory module that injects multi-second history through the vision encoder. Because reasoning and action share a single set of weights, the pretrained VLM's capabilities carry over to physical behavior: the model follows instructions closely, and prompts directly steer action granularity, task horizon, and out-of-distribution scene handling without further training. Pretrained on a large collection of robot datasets together with VQA samples, G0.5 surpasses state-of-the-art models across 7 independent regimes: real-world fine-tuning on R1lite and R1pro robots (76.7\% vs.\ 53.3\% for $π_{0.5}$ and 24.4\% for GR00T-N1.7), the 2025 BEHAVIOR Challenge on 50 long-horizon household mobile manipulation tasks using a generalist policy (31.4\% vs.\ 26.3\% for $π_{0.5}$ and 26.1\% for the challenge winner), DROID post-training followed by zero-shot transfer to an unseen environment and objects (82.5\%), a language-following Pick-and-Place benchmark, LIBERO (98.9\%), RoboTwin 2.0 (93.3\%), and SimplerEnv-Bridge (87.3\%).

</details>


### [190] [Policy-Induced Hand Priors in Humanoid Dual-Arm Manipulation: Diagnosing and Mitigating Initial-Pose Dependence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11769)
*Chaeyeon Jung,Juyoun Park*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了VLA策略在人形双臂操作中对初始姿态的依赖性，提出了HandPriorScore等指标量化策略固有的手部偏好，发现初始姿态与策略之间存在强交互作用，并证明扩充训练数据的初始姿态覆盖率和针对性增强可显著提升鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA策略需要在机器人初始配置变化时保持稳健，但聚合任务成功率可能掩盖特定姿态下的失败和不恰当的手部选择问题，现有研究缺乏对初始姿态依赖性的系统性分析。

Method: 提出HandPriorScore、残差手部偏差和目标响应性三个量化指标；在多个策略和17种初始配置下进行评估，分析初始姿态与策略的交互；通过扩充训练数据初始姿态覆盖率和针对低性能配置的定向增强来验证改进效果。

Result: 发现了强初始姿态-策略交互；特定初始臂配置可抑制或诱发不对称手部偏好且方向和强度因策略而异；腕部相机观测影响手部选择和任务性能；扩大初始姿态覆盖率显著提升鲁棒性，针对低性能配置的增强提高其成功率。

Conclusion: 初始姿态条件化的手部先验被表征，确定局部初始臂配置是手部选择行为的因果控制柄，数据覆盖率和训练组成对初始姿态鲁棒性有显著影响。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) policies are expected to operate robustly across variations in the robot's initial configuration, yet aggregate task success can conceal pose-specific failures and inappropriate hand selection. This work investigates initial-pose dependence in VLA-based humanoid dual-arm manipulation. We characterize the initial-condition-dependent early hand preference as a policy-induced hand prior and quantify it using HandPriorScore, residual hand bias, and target responsiveness. Evaluations across multiple policies and 17 initial configurations reveal strong initial-pose--policy interactions: the same pose produces substantially different success rates across policies, while a single policy exhibits large performance variation across poses. Specific initial arm configurations can suppress or induce an asymmetric hand preference, with the resulting effect varying in direction and strength across policies. Wrist-camera observations also influence hand selection and task performance. Expanding initial-pose coverage in the training dataset substantially improves robustness, while targeted augmentation around a low-performing configuration increases its success rate. Comparisons across training configurations show that sufficient exposure to the target simulation task is beneficial, whereas the effect of real or auxiliary data depends on pose coverage, simulation ratio, and observation availability. These findings characterize a pose-conditioned hand prior, identify a localized initial arm configuration as a causal handle on hand-selection behavior, and demonstrate how data coverage and training composition affect initial-pose robustness.

</details>


### [191] [Enhancing Visual Domain Robustness in Behaviour Cloning via Saliency-Guided Augmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11870)
*Zheyu Zhuang,Ruiyu Wang,Nils Ingelhag,Ville Kyrki,Danica Kragic*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RoboSaGA是一种基于显著性引导的叠加式数据增强方法，专为视觉模仿学习设计。它利用策略驱动的显著性在像素级别动态调整增强强度，在不影响任务关键信息的前提下对非关键区域进行强增强，从而在保持域内性能的同时显著提升对视觉域偏移（如阴影、干扰物、背景和光照变化）的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统图像增强方法（如随机裁剪和颜色抖动）在视觉域偏移较大时效果不佳；叠加式增强方法虽在计算机视觉中展现出泛化潜力，但由于模仿学习中需要保留任务关键语义、时空关系和智能体-目标交互，其适用性尚不明确。因此需要一种既能利用叠加增强优势又不破坏任务关键信息的专门方法。

Method: RoboSaGA属于叠加式（superimposition）增强家族。其核心机制是使用策略驱动的显著性信息（saliency）在像素级别动态调整增强强度：对任务不相关区域施加激进增强，同时对任务关键区域进行保留。该方法无需修改网络结构或引入额外学习目标，可直接集成到现有模仿学习架构中。

Result: 在仿真和真实世界环境中的实验表明，RoboSaGA能够保持域内性能不下降，同时显著提升对多种视觉域偏移的鲁棒性，包括干扰物变化、背景变化、光照变化和阴影变化。

Conclusion: RoboSaGA为视觉模仿学习提供了一种有效的叠加式数据增强方案，解决了传统增强方法在域偏移下泛化不足的问题，且具有即插即用的优点，对实际机器人部署具有实用价值。

Abstract: In vision-based behavior cloning (BC), conventional image augmentations such as Random Crop and Color Jitter often fall short under substantial visual domain shifts, including changes in shadows, distractors, and backgrounds. Superimposition-based augmentations, which blend in-domain and out-of-domain images, have shown promise for improving generalization in computer vision, but their suitability for BC remains uncertain because task-critical semantics, spatiotemporal relationships, and agent-target interactions must be preserved. To address this, we introduce RoboSaGA, a Saliency-Guided Augmentation method within the superimposition family tailored for vision-based BC. RoboSaGA dynamically adjusts augmentation intensity at the pixel level using policy-driven saliency, enabling aggressive augmentation in task-irrelevant regions while preserving task-critical information. It integrates seamlessly into existing architectures without requiring structural modifications or additional learning objectives. Experiments in both simulated and real-world settings show that RoboSaGA preserves in-domain performance while substantially improving robustness to visual domain shifts, including distractor and background changes, as well as lighting and shadow variations. Code is available at https://github.com/Zheyu-Zhuang/RoboSaGA.

</details>


### [192] [D3D-GEN: Robot-Aware Domain-Grounded Interactive 3D World Generation for Social Robotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11876)
*Anh Duc Do,Volodymyr Scherbyna,Tai Duc Nguyen,Spaarsh Thakkar,Zhengcheng Shen,Teham Buiyan,Archan Misra,Linh Kästner*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: D3D-GEN是一个世界生成系统，结合领域代理与检索增强生成（RAG）管道，能够快速生成领域接地、完全交互式的3D仿真环境（如住宅、医院、办公室），支持Isaac Sim和Gazebo模拟器，无需依赖固定3D模型数据库。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身AI社会导航的训练和验证依赖于逼真的仿真环境，但现有方法难以在真实感和可模拟性之间取得平衡。

Method: 系统由领域代理和研究代理组成：研究代理自动收集公开领域特定数据并构建持久化领域数据库；RAG管道动态查询用户提供的语义数据库，生成合理的平面图和物体布局；最终输出可加载到Isaac Sim和Gazebo中的交互式3D世界。

Result: 已为多个常见领域（室内住宅、医院、办公室）构建数据库，并为每个领域生成数十个不同且合理的仿真环境；提供本地Web前端以支持快速交互式世界生成。

Conclusion: D3D-GEN通过自动化的领域知识收集和RAG驱动的世界合成，实现了无需固定模型库的快速、领域多样化的3D仿真环境生成，为机器人社会导航研究提供了高效的工具。

Abstract: Training and validation of Embodied AI for social navigation critically depends on realistic simulation environments, yet many current approaches fail to find a balance between realism and simulability. We propose D3D-GEN, a novel world generation system that combines a domain agent with a retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) pipeline grounded in that domain. Our system enables users to rapidly generate domain-grounded, fully interactive 3D worlds by automating both the collection of domain knowledge and the synthesis of realistic floorplans and object placements, without dependence on any fixed 3D model database. Given a domain description prompt, the research agent collects publicly accessible domain-specific data and constructs a persistent domain database. Using this database, our RAG pipeline generates plausible floorplans and object placements by dynamically querying a user-provided semantic database, which can be easily extended or modified. The output is a fully interactive 3D world loadable by the popular simulators Isaac Sim and Gazebo. With our approach, we have built databases for several common domains (indoor residential, hospital, office) and generated dozens of distinct, plausible simulation environments for each domain. We present D3D-GEN with a local web frontend that facilitates rapid, interactive world generation for robot simulation.

</details>


### [193] [Scalable Multi-Agent Maze Traversal with Local Communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11895)
*Julian Rau,Jahir Argote-Gerald,Grace McFassel,Genki Miyauchi,Paul Trodden,Roderich Groß*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种分布式多智能体算法，使智能体能够在未知且可能含环的图中协作导航，通过领导者-追随者关系和领导者切换机制，每时刻仅一个智能体执行单智能体迷宫求解器进行探索，算法具备完备性，其makespan渐近等价于最优全知策略，并通过多达625个智能体的仿真验证了性能优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 洞穴网络、管道系统等迷宫类环境对多智能体在未知环境且通信受限条件下的导航构成重大挑战，现有方法难以同时保证完备性、高效性和可扩展性。

Method: 提出分布式算法：智能体在指定起始节点顺序进入，通过局部通信和领导者-追随者关系及领导者切换协调，每时刻仅一个探测智能体运行单智能体迷宫求解器遍历未知图，其余智能体避免碰撞并跟随。

Result: 证明了算法的完备性，makespan在智能体数量上渐近等价于最优全知策略；推导了时间和空间复杂度；仿真显示多达625个智能体时平均总燃料消耗随智能体数量增加而降低，且显著优于所有智能体独立执行单智能体求解器的朴素基线。

Conclusion: 该分布式协调策略能有效克服未知迷宫环境中多智能体导航的通信与探索瓶颈，在理论上保证最优渐近性能，在实践中展现良好的可扩展性和燃料效率。

Abstract: Cave networks, pipe systems, and similar maze-like environments pose significant challenges for multi-agent navigation in unknown settings with limited communication. We propose a distributed algorithm that enables agents to collectively traverse an unknown, possibly cyclic graph. Agents enter sequentially at a designated start node and are tasked to localize and reach an undisclosed goal while avoiding collisions. They coordinate via local communication using leader-follower relationships and leader switching. At any moment in time, exploration is performed by only one of the agents, which runs a single-agent maze solver. We prove that the algorithm is complete, that its makespan is asymptotically equivalent (in the number of agents) to that of an optimal full-knowledge strategy, and derive its time and space complexity. Simulations with up to $625$ agents show a decreasing average sum-of-fuels as the number of agents increases and demonstrate that the proposed approach outperforms a naïve baseline in which all agents independently execute the single-agent solver.

</details>


### [194] [DaViNCi: A Dataset Towards Outdoor Vision-and-Language Navigation with Continuous Actions and Dynamic Elements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11901)
*Zihao Xie,Pingrui Lai,Yitong Wu,Hua Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了DaViNCi，首个同时引入连续动作和动态因素的户外视觉语言导航（VLN）数据集，包含6个地图共6,933条轨迹，实验表明在此数据集上成功率显著下降，验证了其在推动户外VLN迈向更真实环境方面的价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有户外VLN数据集依赖固定的离散拓扑图构建，无法适应快速变化的真实户外环境，阻碍了VLN智能体从仿真到真实场景的迁移。

Method: 构建名为DaViNCi的户外VLN数据集，首次同时引入连续动作空间和动态环境因素，智能体需在户外环境中使用连续动作导航，同时应对不可预测的动态元素。

Result: 数据集包含6个不同地图共6,933条轨迹。对比实验发现，该数据集上离散环境的成功率比之前的数据集下降超过10%，连续环境下下降更为显著，并阐明了动作粒度与动态元素的影响。

Conclusion: DaViNCi对现有VLN方法构成显著挑战，证明了其在推动户外VLN向更真实环境发展中的实用价值。

Abstract: Vision-and-Language Navigation (VLN) has progressively expanded from indoor to outdoor environments. However, existing outdoor VLN datasets still rely on fixed discrete topological graphs for construction. It fails to align with the rapidly changing real-world outdoor environments and impedes the sim-to-real transfer of VLN agents. To address this limitation, we propose DaViNCi (\textbf{D}yn\textbf{a}mic \textbf{Vi}sion-and-Language \textbf{N}avigation in \textbf{C}ont\textbf{i}nuous Environment), the first outdoor VLN dataset that simultaneously introduces both continuous and dynamic factors. The agent not only moves in the outdoor environment using continuous actions but is also required to handle unpredictable dynamic elements. The dataset encompasses six distinct maps with a total of 6,933 trajectories. Through comprehensive comparative experiments, we find that the success rate on DaViNCi decreased by more than 10\% in discrete environments compared to previous datasets. And there is an even greater decline in continuous settings, demonstrating the challenge of DaViNCi. Furthermore, we clarify the impact of action granularity and dynamic elements. These results demonstrate the practical value of DaViNCi in advancing outdoor VLN toward more realistic environments. The website is https://xzh0312.github.io/DaViNCi/.

</details>


### [195] [Learning Loco-Manipulation From SMPC Demonstrations With Sparse Offline-to-Online RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12063)
*Martin Schuck,Maks Sorokin,Simone Manni,Duy Ta,Angela P. Schoellig,Marco Hutter,Simon Le Cleac'H,Jan Brüdigam*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种基于样本模型预测控制（SMPC）的自动化专家框架，用于生成大规模离线数据，使稀疏奖励的强化学习（RL）能够高效训练，无需密集奖励塑形。高层RL策略与底层动态稳定性控制器结合，在Sim-to-Real迁移中超越了原始最优控制教师，并在不同形态机器人（Spot四足机器人和G1人形机器人）上成功部署了复杂的腿部操控技能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准强化学习在复杂任务上的扩展受到密集奖励塑形过程缓慢且需要手工设计的严重瓶颈，因此需要一种自动化、快速可调的方法来绕过这一限制。

Method: 在仿真中完全使用样本模型预测控制（SMPC）作为自动化专家，生成大规模离线数据集以解决探索问题；然后使用纯稀疏任务奖励训练离线策略RL智能体；最后将高层智能体与低层动态稳定性控制器集成。

Result: 该框架在多个机器人形态上成功部署复杂的腿部操控技能（Spot四足和G1人形），学习到的策略超越了原始最优控制教师，实现更优的行为且严格符合真实任务目标。

Conclusion: 通过SMPC生成离线数据可以消除对密集奖励塑形的依赖，显著缩短新技能的学习时间，并且结合低层稳定性控制器的Sim-to-Real框架具有良好的鲁棒性和可扩展性。

Abstract: Integrating locomotion and manipulation is essential for robot autonomy, but scaling standard Reinforcement Learning (RL) to complex tasks is severely bottlenecked by the slow, manual process of dense reward shaping. To bypass this limitation, we leverage Sample-based Model Predictive Control (SMPC) entirely in simulation as an automated, rapidly tunable expert to generate massive offline datasets. Because this data solves the fundamental exploration problem, we can train an off-policy RL agent using purely sparse task rewards, drastically reducing the time required to learn new skills and eliminating the need for manual tuning. Integrating this high-level agent with a low-level dynamic stability controller yields more optimal behaviors that strictly align with true task objectives, ultimately allowing the learned policies to surpass the original optimal control teacher. We validate the robustness of this sim-to-real framework by successfully deploying complex loco-manipulation skills across different morphologies, including an arm-equipped Spot quadruped and a G1 humanoid.

</details>


### [196] [HandEdit: A Unified Benchmark for Egocentric Human-to-Robot Dexterous Hand Image Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12122)
*Zhenjie Yang,Xingyu Jiao,Guopeng Zhong,Shuzhe Yang,Shi Che,Chao Wu,Chenyu Jiang,Dongjie Zhang,Yideng Zhang,Zheng Zhang,Muyun Jiang,Haisheng Su,Shuang Jin,Donghang Zhang,Chao Yang,Li Chen,Hongyang Li,Zuxuan Wu,Yu-Gang Jiang,Xiaosong Jia,Junchi Yan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出HandEdit，一个大规模具身感知图像编辑数据集与基准，将人类手部/手臂图像转换为多种灵巧机器人形态，包含超过2亿个编辑实例，覆盖26种URDF配置，并建立了统一的基准评估协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 灵巧手操作是具身智能的核心，但采集具身感知遥操作数据成本高昂。虽然人类第一视角视频可作为可扩展的数据替代方案，但人体与机器人在外观、关节结构和相机视角上的巨大差异阻碍了协同训练，现有通用图像编辑模型缺乏必要的具身先验来弥合这一差距。

Method: 构建HandEdit数据集：从5个不同来源数据集生成超过2亿个编辑实例，覆盖26种不同的URDF模型（13种仅手部+13种手臂组合）。同时建立统一的基准协议，设置Hand-only和Hand-Arm两个轨道的URDF条件化评估，并采用多维指标套件（通用相似度指标、VLM判断、具身感知指标）评估11个代表性图像编辑基线模型。

Result: 对11个代表性图像编辑基线模型进行了广泛评估，使用包含通用相似度指标、VLM判断和具身感知指标的多维指标体系，验证了HandEdit作为图像编辑与机器人学交叉领域关键资源的有效性。

Conclusion: HandEdit作为图像编辑与机器人学交叉领域的关键资源，能够推动具身感知编辑模型的发展，同时支持从丰富的人类视频数据中实现可扩展的灵巧机器人学习，为更通用的具身智能铺平道路。

Abstract: Robotic manipulation with dexterous hands is a cornerstone of Embodied AI, yet its progress is stifled by the high cost of collecting embodiment-aware teleoperation data. While abundant egocentric videos of human hands offer a scalable alternative, the profound discrepancies in appearance, articulation, and camera viewpoints between human and robotic data raise significant challenges for co-training. Though existing general image-editing models demonstrate strong capabilities, they lack necessary embodiment-specific priors to fully bridge this gap. In this work, we present HandEdit, a unified large-scale embodiment-aware image-editing dataset and benchmark specifically designed to transform human hands and arms into various dexterous robotic embodiments within egocentric frames. HandEdit comprises over 200M editing instances derived from five diverse source datasets, covering 26 distinct URDFs, including 13 hand-only and 13 hand-arm configurations. Alongside the dataset, we establish a unified benchmark protocol with two tracks: Hand-only and Hand-Arm, supporting URDF-conditioned evaluation. We conduct extensive evaluations of 11 representative image-editing baselines using a multi-dimensional metric suite, including generic similarity metrics, VLM-based judgment, and embodiment-aware metrics. HandEdit serves as a critical resource at the intersection of image editing and robotics: it advances embodiment-aware editing models while enabling scalable dexterous robotic learning from abundant human video data, paving the way for more generalizable Embodied AI.

</details>


### [197] [Learning-Based Behavior Planning for Automated Driving: Real-World Integration and Deployment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12198)
*Jean-Pierre Busch,Guido Linden,Jan Bergmann,Lutz Eckstein*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种混合规划架构，将深度学习网络（用于复杂交通场景理解和驾驶行为提议）与基于优化的监督层（用于验证提议并强制执行可驾驶性和安全约束）相结合，在真实世界城市数据和实车（研究车辆karl）上进行了验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于学习的运动规划方法在复杂环境中表现优异，但其复杂性和缺乏透明性导致可解释性差、信任度低，且安全保证困难，亟需一种兼顾学习能力与可验证性的方案。

Method: 提出混合规划架构：使用深度神经网络解释复杂交通场景并生成驾驶行为提议，同时构建一个基于优化的监督层来验证该提议，并对可驾驶性和安全约束进行显式强制执行，实现学习与经典方法的优势互补。

Result: 在真实世界城市数据的开环研究中评估了学习规划器的驾驶行为，讨论了闭合回路稳定运行的系统集成问题，并在研究车辆karl上完成了真实世界部署和报道。

Conclusion: 该混合架构能够在发挥机器学习优势的同时，通过优化监督层保证规划的可验证性和安全性，为自动化车辆运动规划的信任度和安全评估提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Recent research in machine and deep learning has shown the potential of learningbased motion planning approaches to improve the driving behavior of automated vehicles, especially in complex environments. However, their complex nature and lack of transparency can hinder explainability and trustworthiness and complicate safety assurance. Motivated by these challenges, we propose a hybrid planning architecture that combines the advantages of machine learning with the verifiability and the determinism of classical approaches. Specifically, we developed a deep neural network to interpret complex traffic scenes and propose driving behavior, while an optimization-based supervision layer validates this proposal and enforces explicit drivability and safety constraints. We evaluate the learned planner's driving behavior in open-loop studies on real-world urban data, discuss system integration aspects for stable closed-loop operation, and report results from real-world deployment on our research vehicle karl..

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [198] [FarSky: Task-Aware Latent-Space Coupling for Generative Intra-Hour Solar Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11254)
*Yann Fabel,Bijan Nouri,Milon Miah,Niklas Blum,Luis F. Zarzalejo,Julia Kowalski,Robert Pitz-Paal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FarSky，一种基于生成式框架的太阳辐照度预测方法。它通过多任务自编码器学习天空图像的共享潜在表示，再使用潜在扩散模型生成未来潜在状态并解码出辐照度预测，通过随机采样获得概率性预测。在西班牙Almería太阳平台的多年度ASI数据集上，FarSky在确定性和概率性预测方面均达到最佳性能，预测技巧提升高达11个百分点，斜坡事件检测F1分数超过60%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准确的太阳辐照度预测对光伏发电并网至关重要。尽管现有深度学习方法已大幅提升预测精度，但大多局限于确定性预测，且难以有效预测斜坡事件（辐照度骤变）。这限制了光伏功率的可靠调度与电网稳定性。

Method: FarSky采用两阶段架构：首先，多任务自编码器学习天空图像的重建与辐照度估计的共享潜在表征；然后，潜在扩散模型在潜在空间中基于近期观测生成未来潜在状态，并直接解码为辐照度预测；通过随机采样天然获得概率性预测。

Result: 在两个独立测试数据集上，FarSky在确定性和概率性预测方面均优于持久性模型、最先进的端到端方法及生成式预测方法，预测技巧提升高达11个百分点；斜坡事件检测性能显著优于现有方法，F1分数超过60%。

Conclusion: 将生成模型与任务感知的潜在空间耦合相结合，能够有效提升太阳辐照度预测的准确性和斜坡事件检测能力，展示了生成式方法在太阳预报领域的巨大潜力。

Abstract: Accurate solar irradiance forecasting is essential for the reliable integration of photovoltaic power into modern electricity grids. All-sky imagers (ASI) provide high-resolution observations of clouds, making them well suited for intra-hour forecasting. Recent deep learning approaches have substantially improved forecast accuracy but are often limited by deterministic predictions and a reduced capability to anticipate ramp events. This work proposes FarSky, a generative forecasting framework that leverages latent-space coupling to learn task-aware representations of sky images. A multi-task autoencoder first learns a shared latent representation for image reconstruction and irradiance estimation. A latent diffusion model then generates future latent states conditioned on recent observations, from which irradiance forecasts are directly decoded. Probabilistic forecasts are inherently obtained through stochastic sampling. The framework is developed using a multi-year ASI dataset acquired at the Plataforma Solar de Almería, Spain, and evaluated on two independent test datasets against persistence, state-of-the-art end-to-end, and generative forecasting approaches. FarSky achieves the best overall deterministic and probabilistic forecasting performance, improving forecast skill by up to 11 percentage points. Furthermore, it substantially improves ramp event detection over existing methods, achieving F1-scores above 60%. These results demonstrate the potential of combining generative models with task-aware latent-space coupling for solar forecasting.

</details>


### [199] [Why AI Detection Fails for Academic Integrity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11256)
*Jonathan A. Karr,Grigorii Khvatskii,Ting Hua,Nitesh V. Chawla*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究发现商业AI检测器无法区分AI辅助编辑与完整LLM草稿，轻量级"仅润色摘要"的合规AI辅助编辑被误判为不当行为的比例高达64-80%，而经过AI人性化工具改写后检测率低于4%，导致诚实使用AI的学生比用规避工具的学生面临更高处罚风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学术机构使用商业AI检测器维护学术诚信，但检测器无法区分AI编辑与全LLM草稿，可能将两者都视为学术不端，需要量化这一政策失败的程度及其不公平后果。

Method: 对已发表的英文摘要进行对照研究，覆盖四个学科领域，比较2013-2015年与2023-2025年两个时间段，使用tau=0.50的人工/AI标签代理，测试Pangram和GPTZero两款检测器，并评估Undetectable AI人性化改写后的规避效果。

Result: 合规的"仅润色摘要"式AI编辑被检测器标记率高达64-80%；未修改的2023-2025原文被标记率为9-15%，非STEM领域远高于STEM领域（p<0.001）；人性化改写后检测率<4%，FNR>96%；检测分数与长token及学术词汇表密度相关，而非单纯反映作者意图。

Conclusion: AI检测器评分不应作为学术不端的独立证据，因为检测器既无法区分合规AI辅助与不当使用，还会系统性惩罚诚实的AI编辑者，同时对人教化规避几乎无效。

Abstract: Institutions use commercial AI detectors for academic integrity, yet detectors cannot distinguish AI editing from full LLM drafts and may treat both as misconduct. In a controlled study of published English abstracts (four domains; 2013 to 2015 vs. 2023 to 2025), we quantify this policy failure under proxy human/AI labels at tau=0.50. Light "refine abstract only" edits, a proxy for guideline-compliant AI assistance, are flagged at 64 to 80% (Pangram/GPTZero). Unmodified 2023 to 2025 originals are flagged at 9 to 15%, with non-STEM rates far above STEM (p<0.001); elevated scores track long-token and Academic Word List density, not authorship intent alone. After Undetectable AI humanization, evasion is near-total: fewer than 4% of AI-labeled rewrites remain flagged (post-humanization detection rate <4%; FNR >96%). Honest AI-editing results in a higher sanction risk than humanizer-assisted evasion. Therefore, detector scores should not serve as standalone misconduct evidence.

</details>


### [200] [Basin: Efficient and Extensible Numerical Optimization in Rust](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11279)
*Johan Larsson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Basin是Rust编程语言的数值优化库，提供统一一致的接口来表述和求解最小化问题，内置广泛的求解器目录并原生支持约束条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数值优化是科学计算中的基础任务，广泛应用于模型拟合、仿真校准、机器学习训练和工程参数优化等领域，需要一个统一的库来简化问题表述和求解过程。

Method: Basin为用户提供单一、一致的方式来表述和求解优化问题，包含广泛的求解器目录，并对约束条件提供一流（first-class）的支持。

Result: 该库在Rust语言中实现了数值优化功能，支持多种求解器和约束处理。

Conclusion: Basin作为Rust生态中的数值优化库，通过统一的接口设计和丰富的求解器支持，降低了数值优化问题的使用门槛。

Abstract: Basin is a numerical optimization library for the Rust programming language. Numerical optimization is the task of finding the inputs that minimize a function, and it is a fundamental element across the sciences: fitting a model to data, calibrating a simulation, training a machine learning model, or choosing engineering parameters that minimize cost. Basin gives users a single, consistent way to both state and solve such problems, with a broad catalog of solvers and first-class support for constraints.

</details>


### [201] [Federated Learning for Distributed CNC Tool Wear Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11281)
*Afsana Khan,Morris Stallmann,Marcin Pietrasik,Charis Kouzinopoulos,Anna Wilbik*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了联邦学习在CNC刀具磨损预测中的应用，通过将刀具轨迹分布到模拟客户端来模拟联邦学习场景，实验结果表明联邦学习性能接近集中式学习且显著优于本地客户端模型，支持分布式CNC制造环境中的协作式刀具磨损预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CNC加工中刀具磨损预测对产品质量和工艺可靠性至关重要，但工业环境中加工数据呈分布式分布且存在数据共享限制，传统机器学习方法难以直接应用，需要一种能在不传输原始数据的情况下进行协作训练的方法。

Method: 采用联邦学习框架进行CNC刀具磨损预测，将刀具轨迹数据分布到多个模拟客户端以模拟联邦学习场景，训练联邦模型并与集中式参考模型及本地客户端基线模型进行性能对比。

Result: 联邦学习模型性能接近集中式学习，显著优于仅使用本地数据的客户端模型。

Conclusion: 联邦学习能够在分布式CNC制造环境中实现有效的协作式刀具磨损预测，在保护数据隐私的同时获得接近集中式训练的性能。

Abstract: Tool wear prediction is an important task in CNC machining, where accurate monitoring of tool condition supports product quality and process reliability. Machine learning methods have shown potential for this task, but their use in industrial environments is limited by the distributed nature of machining data and by restrictions on data sharing between machines, sites, or organizations. Federated learning offers a suitable framework for this setting by enabling collaborative model training without transferring raw operational data. This paper investigates federated learning for CNC tool wear prediction. Tool trajectories are distributed across simulated clients to represent a federated learning scenario. The federated models are compared against centralized references and local client baselines. Results show that federated learning achieves performance close to centralized learning and improves significantly over local client models. These findings indicate that federated learning can support collaborative tool wear prediction in distributed CNC manufacturing environments.

</details>


### [202] [Terminal Symmetry as a Decision Resource: Statewise Refinement for Anytime Verified Construction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11318)
*Yi Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向顺序构造任务的"终态对称性"决策资源视角，通过"运输-细化-验证"框架将过程证据、终态对应和已验证状态相结合，实现高效率的自上而下的构造规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多顺序构造任务在完成时具有精确对称性，但执行过程是方向性且依赖历史的。如何利用这些终态对称性作为决策资源来提升构造效率，是一个未被充分探索的问题。

Method: 提出决策-资源视角的三步分解：过程证据提供方向性，终态对应在等价结果间传递结构，实现状态证据在转换后刷新当前决策相关性，固定验证器认证执行。据此设计method方法，包含episode固定的运输过程结构、状态限制的过程秩、转换后刷新的状态依赖残差秩，以及序秩交汇操作，其top-k集合恰为两个提案前缀的并集。

Result: 在CAD装配、Mini-Programs和精确填充任务中，状态级刷新将任意时刻AUC分别提升6.77、21.75和8.68个百分点。在1,135个GRN OOD场景目标移除episode上，方法在三个规模下均获得最低的平均封顶验证器成本。

Conclusion: 终态对称性可以成为可复用的决策资源，状态级信号在聚合和调度器组织间可迁移，方法在验证器查询界上达到紧的最坏情况最优性。

Abstract: Many sequential construction tasks exhibit exact symmetry at completion while their execution remains directed and history-dependent. We develop a decision-resource view of terminal symmetry: process evidence supplies directionality, terminal correspondence transports that structure across equivalent outcomes, realized-state evidence refines its current decision relevance after transitions, and a fixed verifier certifies execution. This decomposition yields transport--refine--certify. \method{} instantiates the principle with an episode-fixed transported process structure, its state-restricted process rank, a state-dependent residual rank refreshed after accepted transitions, and an ordinal rank meet whose top-$k$ set is exactly the union of the two proposal prefixes. The meet provides a completion guarantee under prefix coverage and attains the tight worst-case verifier-query bound under the corresponding prefix information model; a two-state construction predicts a strict post-transition dynamic--static separation. Across CAD assembly, Mini-Programs, and exact-fill packing, statewise refresh improves anytime AUC by up to $6.77$, $21.75$, and $8.68$ points, respectively. On 1,135 target-removal episodes from the official GRN OOD scenes, \method{} attains the lowest mean capped verifier cost at all three scales among the compared GRN and CDGS-style planners. The statewise signal also transfers across aggregation and scheduler organizations. Terminal symmetry thereby becomes a reusable decision resource for directed construction.

</details>


### [203] [Contextual Quality-Diversity Evolutionary Reinforcement Learning for HVAC Control in Tropical Commercial Buildings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11324)
*Tran Le Vu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为CQD-ERL的上下文质量多样性进化强化学习控制器，用于热带水冷式冷站及其风侧的监督控制，通过维护按运行上下文和不变行为描述符联合索引的专家策略产品档案，在年度回测中优于ASHRAE Guideline 36基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统控制器收敛于单一标量化策略，难以应对热带冷站复杂的天气负载变化和安全约束，需要一种能够保持策略多样性并适应动态运行上下文的智能控制方法。

Method: CQD-ERL采用质量多样性进化强化学习框架，维护一个产品档案，其中专家策略由两个维度联合索引：数据驱动的运行上下文（每日天气和负载模式聚类）和上下文不变的行为描述符。档案由无梯度进化算子和共享回放缓冲区的soft-actor-critic策略梯度算子共同填充，所有动作在执行前均通过确定性安全护盾过滤。

Result: 控制器在代表新加坡商业建筑潜热负荷、冷却塔逼近度和湿度约束的两层降阶环境中训练，并在全年回测中与ASHRAE Guideline 36基线进行对比评估。

Conclusion: CQD-ERL通过上下文感知的多样性策略档案结合安全护盾，为热带冷站监督控制提供了一种能同时兼顾适应性、多样性和安全性的强化学习解决方案。

Abstract: This paper proposes a contextual quality-diversity evolutionary reinforcement-learning controller, CQD-ERL, for the supervisory control of a tropical, water-cooled chiller plant and its associated air side. Rather than converging to a single scalarised policy, the controller maintains a product archive of specialised policies indexed jointly by a data- driven operating context, a cluster of daily weather and load regime, and a context-invariant behaviour descriptor, filled by a gradient-free evolutionary operator and a soft-actor-critic policy-gradient operator that share one replay buffer. Every action is filtered through a deterministic safety shield before execution. The controller is trained on a two-tier reduced-order environment representing the latent load, cooling-tower approach and humidity constraints of a Singapore commercial building, and is evaluated over a full annual backtest against an ASHRAE Guideline 36 baseline.

</details>


### [204] [Long-Horizon Forecasting of Complete Financial Statements with Forma](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11327)
*Travis L. Johnson,Jiannan Jiang,Soumyabrata Chaudhuri,Yihao Chen,Lauren Falvey,Donal O'Cofaigh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ProForma-20Q基准和Forma模型，用于联合预测长达20个季度的完整财务报表（78个科目），在chang-space R²指标上优于各类基线模型，且预测间隔覆盖良好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作未能联合预测超过一年的完整财务报表，而贴现现金流估值中大部分企业价值位于该时间窗口之后，因此需要更长期、更完整的财务报表预测方法。

Method: 构建ProForma-20Q基准，将财务报表表示为（账户、季度、数值）元组集合，提出Forma——一种基于Transformer的模型，通过掩码元组高斯似然进行训练，支持场景分析且无需重训练。

Result: Forma在所有基线上均取得最优性能，包括经典机器学习、链式梯度提升、零样本时序基础模型及前沿大语言模型；优势随预测期限拉长而扩大；高斯预测区间从未欠覆盖；预测几乎满足会计恒等式，且可恢复精确一致性而无显著精度损失。

Conclusion: 专业化的财务报表预测模型（Forma）优于通用大模型和大规模预训练模型，尤其在长期预测场景下表现突出，表明在金融报表预测这一特定任务上，专用架构与目标函数设计比模型规模更具价值。

Abstract: Specialist training beats generalist scale when forecasting financial statements. To our knowledge, no prior work jointly forecasts complete financial statements beyond one year, yet in a discounted-cash-flow valuation most firm value sits past that window. We release ProForma-20Q, a reproducible benchmark for forecasting 78 statement line items 1-20 quarters ahead, for anonymized firms, from past statements and an industry code, scored by change-space $R^2$. On it, Forma, a transformer that reads statements as sets of (account, quarter, value) tuples and maximizes a masked-tuple Gaussian likelihood, beats every competitor we field: classical machine learning, chained gradient boosting, a zero-shot time-series foundation model, and frontier large language models. Its lead widens with horizon, where valuation needs accuracy most, and its Gaussian predictive intervals never under-cover. Forma's forecasts nearly satisfy accounting identities; exact coherence is recoverable at no statistically significant accuracy cost. Its tuple interface supports scenario analysis without retraining, and we show that pinning future revenue paths sharpens the rest of the statement.

</details>


### [205] [Weightless Fine-Tuning: Personalizing LLMs via Logit-Space Transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11342)
*Bohan Zhang,Anqi Ni,Yixin Wang,Paramveer S. Dhillon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Weightless Fine-Tuning (WFT)，一种无需权重更新的训练-free解码时方法，通过跨前缀传输算子近似监督微调（SFT）的分布效果，在个性化任务中以不到7%的计算开销达到接近SFT的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SFT在个性化场景中代价高昂，因为每个作者都需要独立的权重访问、优化、存储和重训练。作者希望通过一种无需权重更新的方法，在解码时近似SFT的分布效果，从而避免这些开销。

Method: WFT在作者的训练序列上计算监督残差，并通过由dropout诱导的跨协方差估计的跨前缀传输算子，将这些残差传输到当前提示。该算子捕获一个上下文中的扰动如何传播到另一个上下文的预测，用logit空间修正替代基于梯度的参数更新。

Result: 在三个LaMP个性化基准上，WFT的平均性能最佳；在单任务上达到或超过SFT；在预算控制对比中，以不到7%的有效计算量接近SFT性能；logit层面分析显示WFT与SFT诱导的logit偏移余弦相似度达0.875。

Conclusion: WFT能够在不需要修改模型权重的情况下捕捉监督适配的分布效果，为个性化场景提供了一种高效且有效的SFT替代方案。

Abstract: Supervised fine-tuning (SFT) is a standard approach for adapting LLMs to a target distribution, but in settings such as personalization, where each author requires separate weight access, optimization, storage, and retraining, its costs become prohibitive. We propose Weightless Fine-Tuning (WFT), a training-free decoding-time method that approximates the distributional effect of SFT without weight updates. WFT computes supervised residuals on an author's training sequence and transports them to the current prompt through a cross-prefix transport operator estimated from dropout-induced cross-covariance. The operator captures how a perturbation at one context propagates to predictions at another, replacing gradient-based parameter updates with logit-space corrections. On three LaMP personalization benchmarks, WFT achieves the best average performance across datasets, matches or exceeds SFT on individual tasks, and outperforms other lightweight baselines on average. In a budget-controlled comparison, WFT approaches SFT performance using less than 7% of the effective computation. Logit-level analysis shows a cosine similarity of 0.875 between the logit shifts induced by WFT and SFT over 95% of the next-token probability mass, suggesting that WFT captures the distributional effect of supervised adaptation without modifying model weights.

</details>


### [206] [Dynamics Models for Offline Hyperparameter Selection in Real-World RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11349)
*Jordan Coblin,Han Wang,Martha White,Adam White*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次将校准模型应用于真实世界的工业场景（市政水处理厂），用于离线超参数选择，验证了其在非平稳、高维传感器数据上的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习在真实系统中部署的主要障碍是超参数选择，尤其在缺少模拟器且在线实验成本高昂的情况下。先前提出的校准模型仅在简单模拟环境中评估过，尚未在真实工业场景中验证。
